📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期人乳头瘤病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 310 名患者 (93%) 的首次 TTMV-HPV DNA 检测呈阴性,3 名患者 (1%) 呈不确定,19 名患者 (6%) 呈阳性。在最初呈阴性结果后再次进行检测的患者中,57 例 (97%) 仍为阴性,2 例 (3%) 转为阳性,这两种阳性转换均与复发相关。在 13 名最初呈阳性结果的患者中,6 名 (46%) 转为阴性且没有人出现复发,而 7 名 (54%) 仍持续呈阳性,6 名 (86%) 出现复发。
  • 调整混杂因素后,四价 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 与所有不良妊娠结局的较低几率相关,与早产(<37 周)、极早产(28 周至 31 周)、自发性早产、早产胎膜早破和严重小于胎龄儿的相关性具有统计学意义。
  • 相互作用组分析确定 CDK1、CCNA2、CAV1、BRCA1、TERT 和 EGFR 为 宿主 中心基因,而 E5_HPV16、E6_HPV33、E7_HPV16、18 和 33 被筛选为病毒中心基因。经过理化筛选,筛选出4条抗病毒药物抗癌肽,与宿主和病毒蛋白对接。肽4的分子动力学模拟证明了所有靶标的对接复合物之间的整体稳定性,MM-GBSA分析表明肽4能够与所有靶蛋白形成能量稳定的复合物。
  • 在这些有序类别中,观察到微核 (p = 0.044)、核芽 (p = 0.029) 和双核细胞 (p = 0.011) 的显着正趋势。 [10.1080/10520295.2026.2732168]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • The first TTMV-HPV DNA test was negative in 310 patients (93%), indeterminate in 3 (1%), and positive in 19 (6%).
  • After adjustment for confounders, quadrivalent human papillomavirus vaccination was associated with lower odds of all included adverse pregnancy outcomes, with statistically significant associations observed for preterm birth (<37 weeks), very preterm birth (28 weeks to 31 weeks), spontaneous preterm birth, preterm prelabour rupture of membranes, and severe small for gestational age infant.
  • Interactome analyses identified CDK1, CCNA2, CAV1, BRCA1, TERT and EGFR as host hub-genes, while E5_HPV16, E6_HPV33, E7_HPV16, 18 and 33 were screened as viral hub-genes.
  • Significant positive trends across these ordered categories were observed for micronuclei (p = 0.044), nuclear buds (p = 0.029), and binucleated cells (p = 0.011). [10.1080/10520295.2026.2732168].

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期人乳头瘤病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 大不里士医科大学的研究人员及其合作者在《病毒学杂志》上发表的一篇叙述性评论讨论了沉默 HPV16 癌基因 E5 和 E6 作为 宫颈癌 治疗策略的潜力。这是基于临床前证据的经证实的科学审查。 [news-f17451db9cdc5fc8]。
  • 一项回顾性研究发现,中国沿海地区三分之一以上的男性门诊患者至少感染一种HPV基因型,其中最常见的是HPV6、HPV52、HPV16、HPV51和HPV42。该研究发表在《BMC 传染病》杂志上。
  • 《英国医学杂志》发表的一项研究发现,接种 HPV 疫苗的女性出现妊娠和分娩并发症的可能性较小,尤其是早产。研究结果表明,怀孕前接种 HPV 疫苗接种 可能会降低早产、胎膜早破、婴儿小于胎龄、死产和新生儿 死亡率 等并发症的风险。
  • 该文章报道了一项研究,该研究表明,据高级编辑 Joshua Fitch 报道,在怀孕前接受 HPV 疫苗 可以减少不良妊娠结局。 [news-fc5dee4784b20b46]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Human papillomaviruses news developments.
  • A narrative review published in Virology Journal by researchers at Tabriz University of Medical Sciences and collaborators discusses the potential of silencing HPV16 oncogenes E5 and E6 as a therapeutic strategy against cervical cancer.
  • A retrospective study found that more than one in three male outpatients in coastal China were infected with at least one HPV genotype, with HPV6, HPV52, HPV16, HPV51, and HPV42 being the most frequently detected.
  • A study published in The BMJ found that women who had been vaccinated against HPV were less likely to experience pregnancy and childbirth complications, particularly preterm birth. [news-4dfb5ea3df77715c].
  • The article reports on a study suggesting that receiving the HPV vaccine before pregnancy is associated with fewer adverse pregnancy outcomes, as reported by Joshua Fitch, Senior Editor. [news-fc5dee4784b20b46].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Head & Neck  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-07  |  DOI: 10.1002/hed.70483  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

治疗 后早期循环肿瘤组织改良病毒 HPV DNA 检测在 HPV 相关口咽癌中的效用

Utility of Early Post‐Treatment Circulating Tumor Tissue Modified Viral HPV DNA Testing in HPV ‐Associated Oropharyngeal Cancer
作者： Scott A. Roof, James Jabalee, Eleni Marie Rettig, Ryan M. Sicard, Rocco M. Ferrandino, Si-Da Chen, Marshall R. Posner, Krzysztof J. Misiukiewicz, Eric Michael Genden, Raymond Liu Chai
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 TTMV-HPV DNA 检测对于 HPV 相关口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 的 治疗 监测 后的预后具有预测作用,但在传统的 3 个月缓解评估之前进行检测的意义仍不清楚。方法 我们回顾性分析了美国 8 个地点的 332 名活检证实 HPV 相关 OPSCC 患者,这些患者接受了初始明确的 治疗,并在 治疗 完成后 1 至 89 天进行了 ≥ 1 次 TTMV-HPV DNA 检测。使用 90 天里程碑方法评估无事件生存期 (EFS)。结果 治疗 后 1-89 天获得的第一次 TTMV-HPV DNA 检测结果,310 名患者 (93%) 呈阴性,3 名患者 (1%) 呈不确定,19 名患者 (6%) 呈阳性。在 59 名最初呈阴性结果后重复检测的患者中,57 名 (97%) 仍为阴性,2 名 (3%) 转为阳性;两名转变的患者随后都出现了复发。在 13 名最初呈阳性结果并进行了足够的轨迹分析随访的患者中,6 名 (46%) 随后转为阴性且没有人出现复发,而 7 名 (54%) 仍持续呈阳性,6 名 (86%) 出现复发。在一项 90 天里程碑分析中,治疗 后早期的最后一个 TTMV-HV DNA 结果与随后的无事件生存相关。结论 治疗 后 1-89 天期间的 TTMV-HPV DNA 检测与 HPV 相关 OPSCC 的后续临床结果相关。在该 队列研究 中,持续的阳性结果表明患者的风险增加,而解决的阳性结果并未出现复发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 治疗 后早期循环肿瘤组织改良病毒 HPV DNA 检测在预测 HPV 相关口咽癌患者临床结果方面的效用。
设计与对象
对美国 8 个地点的 332 名活检证实患有 HPV 相关口咽鳞状细胞癌的患者进行回顾性分析,这些患者在 治疗 完成后 1 至 89 天进行了≥1 次 TTMV-HPV DNA 检测。
核心方法
使用 90 天标志性方法评估无事件生存期,并根据患者的 TTMV-HPV DNA 检测结果对患者进行分类。
主要结果
310 名患者 (93%) 的首次 TTMV-HPV DNA 检测呈阴性,3 名患者 (1%) 呈不确定,19 名患者 (6%) 呈阳性。在最初呈阴性结果后再次进行检测的患者中,57 例 (97%) 仍为阴性,2 例 (3%) 转为阳性,这两种阳性转换均与复发相关。在 13 名最初呈阳性结果的患者中,6 名 (46%) 转为阴性且没有人出现复发,而 7 名 (54%) 仍持续呈阳性,6 名 (86%) 出现复发。
解释与创新
作者解释说,治疗 后早期 TTMV-HPV DNA 检测可以识别复发风险增加的患者,持续的阳性结果与更差的结果相关。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,治疗 后早期 TTMV-HPV DNA 检测可能会改善 监测 和 HPV 相关口咽癌患者的风险分层。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的选择偏倚而受到限制,但由于样本量大且测试结果与临床结果之间存在明确的关联,因此证据充足。

Utility of Early Post‐Treatment Circulating Tumor Tissue Modified Viral HPV DNA Testing in HPV ‐Associated Oropharyngeal Cancer

Authors: Scott A. Roof, James Jabalee, Eleni Marie Rettig, Ryan M. Sicard, Rocco M. Ferrandino, Si-Da Chen, Marshall R. Posner, Krzysztof J. Misiukiewicz, Eric Michael Genden, Raymond Liu Chai
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background TTMV‐HPV DNA testing is prognostic during post‐treatment surveillance of HPV‐associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC), but the significance of testing before conventional 3‐month response assessment remains unclear. Methods We retrospectively analyzed 332 patients across 8 US sites with biopsy‐proven HPV‐associated OPSCC who underwent initial definitive treatment and had ≥ 1 TTMV‐HPV DNA test performed 1 to 89 days after treatment completion. Event‐free survival (EFS) was evaluated using a 90‐day landmark approach. Results The first TTMV‐HPV DNA test obtained 1–89 days after treatment was negative in 310 patients (93%), indeterminate in 3 (1%), and positive in 19 (6%). Among 59 patients with repeat testing after an initially negative result, 57 (97%) remained negative and 2 (3%) converted to positive; both patients who converted subsequently developed recurrence. Of 13 patients with an initially positive result and sufficient follow‐up for trajectory analysis, 6 (46%) subsequently resolved to negative and none developed recurrence, whereas 7 (54%) remained persistently positive and 6 (86%) developed recurrence. In a 90‐day landmark analysis, the last early post‐treatment TTMV‐HV DNA result was associated with subsequent event‐free survival. Conclusions TTMV‐HPV DNA testing during the 1–89‐day post‐treatment interval was associated with subsequent clinical outcomes in HPV‐associated OPSCC. Persistent positivity identified patients at increased risk, whereas positive results that resolved were not followed by recurrence in this cohort.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the utility of early post-treatment circulating tumor tissue modified viral HPV DNA testing in predicting clinical outcomes for patients with HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis of 332 patients with biopsy-proven HPV-associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma across 8 US sites who had ≥1 TTMV-HPV DNA test performed 1 to 89 days after treatment completion.
Core methods
Event-free survival was evaluated using a 90-day landmark approach, and patients were categorized based on their TTMV-HPV DNA test results.
Main results
The first TTMV-HPV DNA test was negative in 310 patients (93%), indeterminate in 3 (1%), and positive in 19 (6%). Among those with repeat testing after an initially negative result, 57 (97%) remained negative and 2 (3%) converted to positive, with both positive conversions associated with recurrence. Of 13 patients with initially positive results, 6 (46%) resolved to negative and none developed recurrence, while 7 (54%) remained persistently positive and 6 (86%) developed recurrence.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that early post-treatment TTMV-HPV DNA testing could identify patients at increased risk of recurrence, with persistent positivity associated with worse outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that early post-treatment TTMV-HPV DNA testing may improve surveillance and risk stratification for patients with HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for selection bias, but the evidence is strong due to the large sample size and clear association between test results and clinical outcomes.

怀孕前四价 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 和妊娠结局:全国范围内基于人群的病例对照研究

Quadrivalent human papillomavirus vaccination before pregnancy and pregnancy outcomes: nationwide, population based case-control study
作者： Zhong-Song Zhang, Alexander Ploner, Verena Sengpiel, Nestory Kasheshi, Bo Jacobsson, Joakim Dillner, Pär Sparén, Cecilia Kärrberg, Jiayao Lei
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估怀孕前四价 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 与随后不良妊娠结局风险之间的关联。
设计与对象
瑞典进行了一项全国性的、基于人群的匹配病例对照研究,覆盖了 2006 年至 2023 年间 16-35 岁未生育妇女中的 624 713 名单胎婴儿。
核心方法
使用条件 Logistic 回归评估 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 年龄的效应修正,以估计比值比并使用 95% 置信区间 (CI) 调整比值比。
主要结果
调整混杂因素后,四价 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 与所有不良妊娠结局的较低几率相关,与早产(<37 周)、极早产(28 周至 31 周)、自发性早产、早产胎膜早破和严重小于胎龄儿的相关性具有统计学意义。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明怀孕前四价 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 与不良妊娠结局风险较低相关,特别是在较早年龄接种疫苗的女性中,这将 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 的益处扩展到癌症预防之外。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,怀孕前四价 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 可能对孕产妇和胎儿健康有额外的好处,这可以为 疫苗接种 计划的公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项全国性的、以人群为基础的病例对照研究,样本量较大,但证据是观察性的,无法确定因果关系。由于潜在的混杂因素,研究结果的可信度中等。

Quadrivalent human papillomavirus vaccination before pregnancy and pregnancy outcomes: nationwide, population based case-control study

Authors: Zhong-Song Zhang, Alexander Ploner, Verena Sengpiel, Nestory Kasheshi, Bo Jacobsson, Joakim Dillner, Pär Sparén, Cecilia Kärrberg, Jiayao Lei
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To evaluate the association between quadrivalent human papillomavirus vaccination before pregnancy and subsequent risk of adverse pregnancy outcomes. DESIGN: Nationwide, population based matched case-control study. SETTING: Population of Sweden, from 1 January 2006 to 31 December 2023. PARTICIPANTS: 624 713 singleton births among nulliparous women aged 16-35 years in Sweden between 2006 and 2023; 92 620 (14.8%) women had received quadrivalent human papillomavirus vaccination before pregnancy. MAIN OUTCOME MEASURES: Risk of adverse pregnancy outcomes associated with quadrivalent human papillomavirus vaccination, with preterm birth (<37 weeks), including its subtypes, and spontaneous preterm birth as primary outcomes and preterm prelabour rupture of membranes, prelabour rupture of membranes, small for gestational age infant (<10th centile), severe small for gestational age infant (<3rd centile), stillbirth, and neonatal mortality as secondary outcomes. Effect modification by age at human papillomavirus vaccination was assessed. Conditional logistic regression was used to estimate odds ratios and adjusted odds ratios with 95% confidence intervals (CIs). RESULTS: After adjustment for confounders, quadrivalent human papillomavirus vaccination was associated with lower odds of all included adverse pregnancy outcomes. Statistically significant associations were observed for preterm birth (<37 weeks) (adjusted odds ratio 0.95, 95% CI 0.91 to 0.99), very preterm birth (28 weeks to 31 weeks) (0.85, 0.75 to 0.97), spontaneous preterm birth (0.95, 0.91 to 0.99), preterm prelabour rupture of membranes (0.93, 0.87 to 1.00), and severe small for gestational age infant (0.92, 0.87 to 0.97). Risk reduction was generally more pronounced among women vaccinated at younger ages. CONCLUSIONS: Quadrivalent human papillomavirus vaccination before pregnancy was associated with a lower risk of adverse pregnancy outcomes, particularly among women vaccinated at earlier ages, extending benefits of human papillomavirus vaccination beyond cancer prevention.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the association between quadrivalent human papillomavirus vaccination before pregnancy and subsequent risk of adverse pregnancy outcomes.
Design and population
A nationwide, population based matched case-control study was conducted in Sweden, covering 624 713 singleton births among nulliparous women aged 16-35 years between 2006 and 2023.
Core methods
Effect modification by age at human papillomavirus vaccination was assessed using conditional logistic regression to estimate odds ratios and adjusted odds ratios with 95% confidence intervals (CIs).
Main results
After adjustment for confounders, quadrivalent human papillomavirus vaccination was associated with lower odds of all included adverse pregnancy outcomes, with statistically significant associations observed for preterm birth (<37 weeks), very preterm birth (28 weeks to 31 weeks), spontaneous preterm birth, preterm prelabour rupture of membranes, and severe small for gestational age infant.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that quadrivalent human papillomavirus vaccination before pregnancy is associated with a lower risk of adverse pregnancy outcomes, particularly among women vaccinated at earlier ages, extending benefits of human papillomavirus vaccination beyond cancer prevention.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that quadrivalent human papillomavirus vaccination before pregnancy may have additional benefits for maternal and fetal health, which could inform public health strategies for vaccination programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a nationwide, population based case-control study with a large sample size, but the evidence is observational and cannot establish causality. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to potential confounding factors.

Journal: Vaccine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-05  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.vaccine.2026.129194  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 93, 129194

基于漫画书的教育干预对尼日利亚 9-14 岁女孩 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 知识和安全认知的影响

Effect of a comic book-based educational intervention on human papillomavirus vaccine knowledge and safety perception among girls aged 9–14 in Nigeria
作者： James Charles Dickerson, Praise Adeyemo, Nicole Kim, Edoghogho Ighodaro, Paulette Ibeka, Olufunke Fasawe, Maryanne W. Chege, Kyra G. Warshaw, Ladan Aliyu, Jane A. Craycroft
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 是女性死亡的主要原因,可以通过 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 和定期筛查来预防。然而,许多低收入和中等收入国家对 疫苗 和筛查的认识较差。
设计与对象
这项单组研究评估了捆绑式教育干预对 HPV 知识和安全认知的影响。该研究在尼日利亚四个州进行,共涉及 396 名 9 至 14 岁的女孩。
核心方法
干预措施由三部分组成,包括在全校范围内为儿童举行集会和讲座,以文化定制的漫画书为指导,向学校的所有儿童分发漫画书,以及由成人社区领袖组成的单独焦点小组。主要结果是干预后六周 9-14 岁女孩 HPV 知识评分的变化。
主要结果
我们发现所有四个州的知识都有显着增长(n = 396;p < 0.01)。基线知识较低的班级从干预中获得更多(p < 0.01)。对 疫苗 安全性的看法从 62% 增加到 81% (p < 0.01)。我们没有发现学习增加和 疫苗接种 收据之间存在关系 (p = 0.16)。
解释与创新
在对尼日利亚 9-14 岁女孩进行教育漫画干预后,人们对 HPV 疫苗 安全性的认识和积极看法有所提高。漫画可能是一种低成本、可扩展且具有文化适应性的健康教育工具。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,使用文化定制漫画书进行教育干预可以提高尼日利亚女孩的 HPV 知识和安全意识,这可能支持制定有效的公共卫生策略以促进 疫苗。
局限与证据强度
该研究是单臂设计,限制了与对照组比较结果的能力。证据是中等的,因为结果是基于自我报告的知识和感知,没有客观的测量。

Effect of a comic book-based educational intervention on human papillomavirus vaccine knowledge and safety perception among girls aged 9–14 in Nigeria

Authors: James Charles Dickerson, Praise Adeyemo, Nicole Kim, Edoghogho Ighodaro, Paulette Ibeka, Olufunke Fasawe, Maryanne W. Chege, Kyra G. Warshaw, Ladan Aliyu, Jane A. Craycroft
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Cervical cancer is a leading cause of death for women and is preventable with the human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine and regular screening. However, awareness of vaccines and screenings is poor in many low- and middle-income countries. In 2023, Nigeria sought to deliver 15 million doses of the HPV vaccine to girls aged 9-14. Within this vaccination campaign, we studied the effect of a comic book educational intervention on knowledge and perception of HPV, cervical cancer, and vaccine safety in girls eligible for the vaccine. METHODS: This single-arm study evaluated the effect of a bundled educational intervention on HPV knowledge and safety perception. The three-part intervention was a schoolwide assembly and lecture for children using a culturally tailored comic book as a guide, distribution of the comic book to all the children at the school and then separate focus groups with adult community leaders. The primary outcome was the change in the HPV knowledge score in girls aged 9-14 six weeks post-intervention. Exploratory outcomes were the differential effect of low versus high baseline knowledge, vaccine safety perception, and change in vaccine receipt status at the class level. RESULTS: We found significant gains in knowledge across all four states (n = 396; p < 0.01). Classes with lower baseline knowledge gained more from the intervention (p < 0.01). Perception of vaccine safety increased from 62% to 81% (p < 0.01). We did not find a relationship between increased learning and vaccination receipt (p = 0.16). CONCLUSION: Awareness and positive perception of the safety of the HPV vaccine increased following an educational comic book intervention in girls aged 9-14 across Nigeria. Comics may be a low-cost, scalable, and culturally adaptable tool for health education.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer is a leading cause of death for women and is preventable with the human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine and regular screening. However, awareness of vaccines and screenings is poor in many low- and middle-income countries.
Design and population
This single-arm study evaluated the effect of a bundled educational intervention on HPV knowledge and safety perception. The study was conducted in four states in Nigeria, involving a total of 396 girls aged 9-14.
Core methods
The three-part intervention included a schoolwide assembly and lecture for children using a culturally tailored comic book as a guide, distribution of the comic book to all the children at the school, and separate focus groups with adult community leaders. The primary outcome was the change in the HPV knowledge score in girls aged 9-14 six weeks post-intervention.
Main results
We found significant gains in knowledge across all four states (n = 396; p < 0.01). Classes with lower baseline knowledge gained more from the intervention (p < 0.01). Perception of vaccine safety increased from 62% to 81% (p < 0.01). We did not find a relationship between increased learning and vaccination receipt (p = 0.16).
Interpretation and novelty
Awareness and positive perception of the safety of the HPV vaccine increased following an educational comic book intervention in girls aged 9-14 across Nigeria. Comics may be a low-cost, scalable, and culturally adaptable tool for health education.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that educational interventions using culturally tailored comic books can improve HPV knowledge and safety perception among girls in Nigeria, which may support the development of effective public health strategies for vaccine promotion.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was a single-arm design, which limits the ability to compare outcomes with a control group. The evidence is moderate as the results are based on self-reported knowledge and perception without objective measures.

埃塞俄比亚西北部健康和社会科学女学生关于 人乳头瘤病毒 的知识、态度和预防实践:一项比较横断面研究

Knowledge, attitudes, and prevention practices regarding human papillomavirus among health and social science female students in Northwest Ethiopia: A comparative cross-sectional study
作者： Birhanemaskal Malkamu, Abiyu Ayalew, Addisu Melake, Alemie Fentie, Ayenew Assefa, Ayenew Berhan, Birhanu Getie, Birhanu Malede, Biruk Legese, Dagnaw Mersha Tegegne
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
大学生对于预防人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 至关重要,但学科之间的比较数据仍然有限。这项研究比较了德布雷塔博尔大学女性健康和社会科学专业学生的 HPV 知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。
设计与对象
2025 年 6 月至 7 月,在埃塞俄比亚西北部的 Debre Tabor 大学进行了一项基于机构的比较横断面研究。主要结果指标:HPV 知识(好与差)、态度(好与差)和 疫苗接种 实践(好与差)。
核心方法
设计:基于机构的比较横断面研究。多变量逻辑回归确定了不良结果的独立预测因素。
主要结果
与社会科学学生 (SSS) 相比,健康科学学生 (HSS) 的认知度更高(83.6% 比 56.7%),态度更积极(57.3% 比 37.6%)。两组均表现出知识匮乏:61.2% 的 HSS 和 80.9% 的 SSS 在知识问题上得分 <50%,且 疫苗接种 率极低(HSS:18.2%;SSS:15.2%)。
解释与创新
For HSS, lack of prior awareness was the strongest predictor of poor practice (AOR = 7.61, 95% CI = 1.65-35.12). For SSS, second-year students had 3.39 times higher odds of poor practice compared to seniors (AOR = 3.39; 95% CI: 1.04-10.99).
科研与公卫意义
无论研究领域如何,都存在深刻的知识与实践差距。SSS 中对社交媒体的高度依赖和中级学生中社交媒体的低实践表明,以大学为基础的干预措施可能有助于国家的努力,尽管实现人口层面的目标需要多部门战略。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Knowledge, attitudes, and prevention practices regarding human papillomavirus among health and social science female students in Northwest Ethiopia: A comparative cross-sectional study

Authors: Birhanemaskal Malkamu, Abiyu Ayalew, Addisu Melake, Alemie Fentie, Ayenew Assefa, Ayenew Berhan, Birhanu Getie, Birhanu Malede, Biruk Legese, Dagnaw Mersha Tegegne
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: University students are vital for Human Papilloma Virus (HPV) prevention, yet comparative data between academic disciplines remain limited. This study compared HPV knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) among female health and Social Science students at Debre Tabor University. OBJECTIVE: To compare HPV knowledge, attitudes, and prevention practices (KAP) among female Health and Social Science students and identify sociodemographic predictors of these outcomes. METHODS: Design: Institutional-Based, comparative cross-sectional study. Setting: Debre Tabor University, Northwest Ethiopia, conducted from June to July 2025. MAIN OUTCOME MEASURES: HPV knowledge (good vs. poor), attitudes (good vs. poor), and vaccination practices (good vs. Multivariable logistic regression identified independent predictors of poor outcomes. RESULTS: Health science students (HSS) reported higher awareness (83.6% vs. 56.7%) and more positive attitudes (57.3% vs. 37.6%) than Social Science students (SSS). However, both groups exhibited poor knowledge: 61.2% of HSS and 80.9% of SSS scored <50% on knowledge questions, and critically low vaccination rates (HSS: 18.2%; SSS: 15.2%). For HSS, lack of prior awareness was the strongest predictor of poor practice (AOR = 7.61, 95% CI = 1.65-35.12). For SSS, second-year students had 3.39 times higher odds of poor practice compared to seniors (AOR = 3.39; 95% CI: 1.04-10.99). SSS predominantly relied on social media (45.0%), while HSS used academic sources (67.5%). CONCLUSION: A profound knowledge-practice gap exists regardless of the field of study. High reliance on social media among SSS and low practice among mid-level students suggest that university-based interventions could potentially contribute to national efforts, though achieving population-level targets requires multi-sectoral strategies..

受访者报告的护士协助、自行收集的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 宫颈癌 成人智力和发育障碍筛查模型的可接受性和耐受性:初步报告

Respondent-Reported Acceptability and Tolerability of a Nurse-Assisted, Self-Collected Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Cervical Cancer Screening Model in Adults With Intellectual and Developmental Disabilities: A Preliminary Report
作者： Zehra Rahman, Jusil Rice, Linda Edwards, Emma Sanad, Rafik Jacob
摘要中文翻译背景和目标 患有智力和发育障碍 (IDD) 的成年人接受 宫颈癌 筛查的比率远低于非残疾成年人。沟通差异、感官敏感性以及难以忍受基于窥器的检查都是造成这种情况的原因。推迟筛查经常被记录为患者拒绝,而不是对适应性护理的需求未得到满足。对自行采集的阴道标本进行 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 检测现已被认为是一种可接受的筛查选择,但针对该人群发布的实施策略有限。本研究的目的是评估受访者报告的接受度、耐受性以及在为成人 IDD 患者提供服务的跨学科初级保健诊所中护士协助自行收集 HPV 筛查模型所需的帮助水平。方法 我们在佛罗里达大学健康中心杰克逊维尔分校的智力和发育障碍成人项目 (PAIDD) 中进行了一项前瞻性、单中心、最低风险的观察性研究。用于 HPV 检测的阴道标本由患者在护士指导下在诊所采集,并根据需要由经过培训的计划护士或护理人员提供实际帮助。该模型还包括结构化的就诊前规划、动画患者教育视频和护理人员参与。REDCap 进行的结构化访问后调查评估了援助的水平和适当性、患者的耐受性、可观察到的痛苦、可接受性以及对未来使用的支持。该调查被设计为护理人员代理工具,并在可能的情况下由患者直接完成;因此,结果被描述为受访者报告的,并明确区分代理和直接答复。结果报告为 n (%)。招募工作正在进行中,计划招募 30 人,这些只是初步调查结果。结果 在 23 名被确定为合格的患者中,有 18 名 (78.3%) 获得了该途径。其中,14家(77.8%)已完成催收,4家(22.2%)拒绝;零 (0%) 次尝试收集但未完成。在被确定符合资格的人中,23 人中有 14 人(60.9%)接受了。所有 14 个样本 (100%) 均返回阴性 高危型HPV 结果。14 个已完成的收集中,有 13 个提供了完整的调查数据。在这 13 次遭遇中,有 11 次(84.6%)的容忍度被评为非常好,有 2 次(15.4%)的容忍度被评为良好,零次(0%)被评为一般、较差或非常差;12 人 (92.3%) 没有观察到痛苦,1 人 (7.7%) 没有观察到痛苦,零人 (0%) 中度或严重痛苦。所有 13 次遇到可用援助数据的情况 (100%) 都需要实际援助,其中 9 次 (69.2%) 由护士提供,4 次 (30.8%) 由护理人员提供;零 (0%) 人在没有帮助或仅通过口头指导的情况下完成,零 (0%) 人在有帮助的情况下仍无法完成。所有 13 名受访者 (100%) 均认为援助水平适当,认为流程可接受或非常可接受,并支持再次提供援助。结论 护士协助的自我收集 HPV 筛查模型对于 IDD 成人学术初级保健诊所的受访者具有良好的耐受性和高度接受性。每次遇到可用的援助数据都需要实际援助,这表明护理支持是该模型的组成部分而不是偶然的。临床效率、医生时间和可扩展性没有被测量,并且仍然是未来评估的假设。招募仍在继续。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
患有智力和发育障碍 (IDD) 的成年人接受 宫颈癌 筛查的比率远低于无残疾的成年人。该研究旨在评估护士协助、自行收集的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 宫颈癌 筛查模型在该人群中的可接受性和耐受性。
设计与对象
佛罗里达大学健康中心杰克逊维尔分校的智力和发育障碍成人项目 (PAIDD) 进行了一项前瞻性、单中心、最低风险观察性研究。该研究包括 23 名符合条件的参与者,其中 18 人提供了筛选途径,14 人完成了收集过程。
核心方法
用于 HPV 检测的阴道标本由患者在护士指导下在诊所采集,并根据需要由经过培训的计划护士或护理人员提供实际帮助。REDCap 进行的结构化访问后调查评估了援助的水平和适当性、患者的耐受性、可观察到的痛苦、可接受性以及对未来使用的支持。该调查被设计为护理人员代理工具,并在可能的情况下由患者直接完成。
主要结果
在 23 名符合资格的参与者中,有 18 名(78.3%)获得了筛选途径,14 名(77.8%)完成了收集过程。在被确定符合资格的人中,23 人中有 14 人(60.9%)接受了。所有 14 个样本均返回阴性 高危型HPV 结果。在获得可用援助数据的 13 次遭遇中,全部需要亲自动手援助,其中 9 次(69.2%)由护士提供,4 次由护理人员提供(30.8%)。所有 13 名受访者均认为援助水平适当,对流程进行了可接受或非常可接受的评价,并支持再次提供援助。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解读为,护士协助的自我收集 HPV 筛查模型对于 IDD 成人学术初级保健诊所的受访者具有良好的耐受性和高度可接受性。该模型解决了该人群未满足的适应性护理需求。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,护士协助的自我收集 HPV 筛查可以通过解决沟通差异、感觉敏感性和难以忍受基于窥器的检查来提高 IDD 成人的 宫颈癌 筛查率。这可以为未来的实施策略提供信息,并加强 监测 和预防工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究是初步的,招募工作正在进行中,计划招募 30 名受试者。没有测量诊所效率、医生时间和可扩展性,这些仍然是未来评估的假设。由于样本量有限和研究的观察性质,证据强度中等。

Respondent-Reported Acceptability and Tolerability of a Nurse-Assisted, Self-Collected Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Cervical Cancer Screening Model in Adults With Intellectual and Developmental Disabilities: A Preliminary Report

Authors: Zehra Rahman, Jusil Rice, Linda Edwards, Emma Sanad, Rafik Jacob
Original abstract (English)
Background and objective Adults with intellectual and developmental disabilities (IDD) are screened for cervical cancer at substantially lower rates than adults without disabilities. Communication differences, sensory sensitivities, and difficulty tolerating speculum-based examination all contribute. Deferred screening is frequently recorded as patient refusal rather than as an unmet need for adapted care. Human papillomavirus (HPV) testing on self-collected vaginal specimens is now recognized as an acceptable screening option, but published implementation strategies for this population are limited. The objective of this study was to evaluate respondent-reported acceptability, tolerability, and level of assistance required for a nurse-assisted self-collected HPV screening model in an interdisciplinary primary care clinic serving adults with IDD. Methods We conducted a prospective, single-site, minimal-risk observational study at the Program for Adults with Intellectual and Developmental Disabilities (PAIDD) at University of Florida Health Jacksonville. Vaginal specimens for HPV testing were collected in the clinic by the patient under nurse guidance, with hands-on assistance from a trained program nurse or a caregiver as needed. The model also included structured pre-visit planning, an animated patient education video, and caregiver engagement. A structured post-visit survey administered in REDCap assessed the level and appropriateness of assistance, patient tolerance, observable distress, acceptability, and support for future use. The survey was designed as a caregiver proxy instrument and was completed directly by the patient where able; outcomes are therefore described as respondent-reported, with proxy and direct responses distinguished explicitly. Results are reported as n (%). Recruitment is ongoing toward a planned enrollment of 30, and these are preliminary findings. Results Of 23 patients identified as eligible, 18 (78.3%) were offered the pathway. Among those offered, 14 (77.8%) completed collection, and four (22.2%) declined; zero (0%) attempted collection without completing it. Uptake was 14 of 23 (60.9%) among those identified as eligible. All 14 specimens (100%) returned negative high-risk HPV results. Complete survey data were available for 13 of the 14 completed collections. Among these 13 encounters, tolerance was rated very good in 11 (84.6%) and good in two (15.4%), with zero (0%) rated fair, poor, or very poor; no distress was observed in 12 (92.3%) and mild distress in one (7.7%), with zero (0%) moderate or severe. All 13 encounters with available assistance data (100%) required hands-on assistance, provided by the nurse in nine (69.2%) and by a caregiver in four (30.8%); zero (0%) were completed unaided or with verbal coaching alone, and zero (0%) were unable to be completed despite assistance. All 13 respondents (100%) judged the level of assistance appropriate, rated the process acceptable or very acceptable, and would support offering it again. Conclusions A nurse-assisted self-collected HPV screening model was well tolerated and highly acceptable to respondents in an academic primary care clinic for adults with IDD. Every encounter with available assistance data required hands-on assistance, indicating that nursing support is integral to the model rather than incidental. Clinic efficiency, physician time, and scalability were not measured and remain hypotheses for future evaluation. Recruitment continues.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Adults with intellectual and developmental disabilities (IDD) are screened for cervical cancer at substantially lower rates than adults without disabilities. The study aimed to evaluate the acceptability and tolerability of a nurse-assisted, self-collected human papillomavirus (HPV) cervical cancer screening model in this population.
Design and population
A prospective, single-site, minimal-risk observational study was conducted at the Program for Adults with Intellectual and Developmental Disabilities (PAIDD) at University of Florida Health Jacksonville. The study included 23 eligible participants, with 18 offered the screening pathway and 14 completing the collection process.
Core methods
Vaginal specimens for HPV testing were collected in the clinic by the patient under nurse guidance, with hands-on assistance from a trained program nurse or a caregiver as needed. A structured post-visit survey administered in REDCap assessed the level and appropriateness of assistance, patient tolerance, observable distress, acceptability, and support for future use. The survey was designed as a caregiver proxy instrument and was completed directly by the patient where able.
Main results
Of 23 eligible participants, 18 (78.3%) were offered the screening pathway, and 14 (77.8%) completed the collection process. Uptake was 14 of 23 (60.9%) among those identified as eligible. All 14 specimens returned negative high-risk HPV results. Among the 13 encounters with available assistance data, all required hands-on assistance, with the nurse providing it in nine (69.2%) and a caregiver in four (30.8%). All 13 respondents judged the level of assistance appropriate, rated the process acceptable or very acceptable, and would support offering it again.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that the nurse-assisted self-collected HPV screening model was well tolerated and highly acceptable to respondents in an academic primary care clinic for adults with IDD. This model addresses the unmet need for adapted care in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that nurse-assisted self-collected HPV screening could improve cervical cancer screening rates among adults with IDD by addressing communication differences, sensory sensitivities, and difficulty tolerating speculum-based examinations. This could inform future implementation strategies and enhance surveillance and prevention efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is preliminary, with recruitment ongoing toward a planned enrollment of 30. Clinic efficiency, physician time, and scalability were not measured, and these remain hypotheses for future evaluation. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the limited sample size and the observational nature of the study.

PAX1和JAM3甲基化对绝经前和绝经后妇女宫颈上皮内瘤变的鉴别诊断价值:中国回顾性临床研究。

Differential diagnostic value of PAX1 and JAM3 methylation for cervical intraepithelial neoplasia between premenopausal and postmenopausal women: a retrospective clinical study in China.
作者： Pei-Xin Wang, Meng-Yu Zhang, Yu-Cheng Lai, Tao Song, Yan Shen, Hai-Ying Chen, Xiao-Yan Chen, 陈海迎
摘要中文翻译背景:PAX1/JAM3 甲基化是高级别宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN) 检测的有前途的生物标志物,但绝经状态可能会影响诊断准确性。这项回顾性研究比较了接受阴道镜检查的绝经前和绝经后女性的临床表现。方法:705 名因筛查结果和/或临床症状异常而接受阴道镜检查的女性按绝经状态进行分层,其中包括 192 名绝经后女性和 513 名绝经前女性。根据组织病理学将受试者进一步分为≤CIN1和CIN2+组。比较绝经前和绝经后女性之间 PAX1/JAM3 甲基化在识别 CIN2+ 病变方面的诊断和分类效用。结果:与绝经前女性相比,PAX1/JAM3 甲基化在绝经后女性 CIN2+ 检测方面表现出优异的诊断性能,无论是单独使用还是组合使用。绝经后女性 PAX1m 和 JAM3m 的敏感性(90.00%,95% CI:76.34-97.21%;82.50%,95% CI:67.22-92.66%)高于绝经前女性(61.98%,95% CI: 52.71-70.65%;60.33%,95% CI:51.04-69.11%)(所有 p < 0.05)。该组合将绝经前女性的敏感性提高至 72.73%,绝经后女性的敏感性维持在 92.50%。此外,两组中 PAX1/JAM3 甲基化均优于细胞学和 HPV16/18 检测(p < 0.001)。在 HPV16/18 阳性或细胞学分诊中,甲基化增加了 CIN2+/CIN3+ 的阳性预测值 (PPV),并减少了阴道镜检查转诊。然而,绝经前女性的 CIN2+ 漏诊率(HPV16/18 阳性分诊为 27.54%,细胞学分诊为 23.85%)高于绝经后女性。结论:绝经前和绝经后接受阴道镜检查的女性中,PAX1/JAM3 甲基化对高级别宫颈病变的诊断性能存在明显差异。应用甲基化生物标志物时应考虑更年期状况,特别是作为提高老年女性诊断准确性的宝贵辅助工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
PAX1/JAM3 甲基化是高级别宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN) 检测的有前途的生物标志物,但绝经状态可能会影响诊断准确性。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究比较了接受阴道镜检查的绝经前和绝经后女性 PAX1/JAM3 甲基化的临床表现。根据组织病理学将受试者进一步分为≤CIN1和CIN2+组。
核心方法
705 名因异常筛查结果和/或临床症状而接受阴道镜检查的女性按绝经状态进行分层,其中包括 192 名绝经后女性和 513 名绝经前女性。比较绝经前和绝经后女性之间 PAX1/JAM3 甲基化用于识别 CIN2+ 病变的诊断和分类效用。
主要结果
与绝经前女性相比,PAX1/JAM3 甲基化在绝经后女性的 CIN2+ 检测中表现出优越的诊断性能,无论是单独使用还是组合使用。绝经后女性 PAX1m 和 JAM3m 的敏感性(90.00%,95% CI:76.34-97.21%;82.50%,95% CI:67.22-92.66%)高于绝经前女性(61.98%,95% CI: 52.71-70.65%;60.33%,95% CI:51.04-69.11%)(所有 p < 0.05)。该组合将绝经前女性的敏感性提高至 72.73%,绝经后女性的敏感性维持在 92.50%。此外,两组中 PAX1/JAM3 甲基化均优于细胞学和 HPV16/18 检测(p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,绝经前和绝经后接受阴道镜检查的女性之间,PAX1/JAM3 甲基化对高级宫颈病变的诊断性能存在明显差异。这一发现强调了在应用甲基化生物标志物时需要考虑绝经状态。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,PAX1/JAM3 甲基化可能是提高老年女性诊断准确性的有价值的辅助工具,特别是在宫颈上皮内瘤变检测的背景下。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的,可能会引入偏差并限制研究结果的普遍性。由于缺乏随机对照试验和潜在的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Differential diagnostic value of PAX1 and JAM3 methylation for cervical intraepithelial neoplasia between premenopausal and postmenopausal women: a retrospective clinical study in China.

Authors: Pei-Xin Wang, Meng-Yu Zhang, Yu-Cheng Lai, Tao Song, Yan Shen, Hai-Ying Chen, Xiao-Yan Chen, 陈海迎
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: PAX1/JAM3 methylation are promising biomarkers for high-grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN) detection, but menopausal status may affect diagnostic accuracy. This retrospective study compared their clinical performance between premenopausal and postmenopausal women undergoing colposcopy. METHODS: 705 women undergoing colposcopy for abnormal screening results and/or clinical symptoms were stratified by menopausal status, including 192 postmenopausal and 513 premenopausal women. Subjects were further classified into ≤CIN1 and CIN2+ groups according to histopathology. Diagnostic and triage utility of PAX1/JAM3 methylation for identifying CIN2+ lesions were compared between premenopausal and postmenopausal women. RESULTS: PAX1/JAM3 methylation demonstrated superior diagnostic performance for CIN2+ detection in postmenopausal women compared with premenopausal women, either alone or in combination. Sensitivities of PAX1m and JAM3m in postmenopausal women (90.00%, 95% CI: 76.34-97.21%; 82.50%, 95% CI: 67.22-92.66%) were higher than those in premenopausal women (61.98%, 95% CI: 52.71-70.65%; 60.33%, 95% CI: 51.04-69.11%) (all p < 0.05). The combination improved sensitivity to 72.73% in premenopausal women and maintained 92.50% in postmenopausal women. Besides, PAX1/JAM3 methylation outperformed cytology and HPV16/18 testing in both groups (p < 0.001). In HPV16/18-positive or cytology triage, methylation increased positive predictive values (PPVs) for CIN2+/CIN3+ and reduced colposcopy referrals . However, CIN2+ missed rates in premenopausal women (27.54% for HPV16/18-positive triage and 23.85% for cytology triage) were higher than those in postmenopausal counterparts. CONCLUSIONS: The diagnostic performance of PAX1/JAM3 methylation for high-grade cervical lesions differs distinctly between premenopausal and postmenopausal women undergoing colposcopy. Menopausal status should be considered when applying methylation biomarkers, particularly as a valuable auxiliary tool to improve diagnostic accuracy in elderly women.

Question and background
PAX1/JAM3 methylation are promising biomarkers for high-grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN) detection, but menopausal status may affect diagnostic accuracy.
Design and population
This retrospective study compared the clinical performance of PAX1/JAM3 methylation between premenopausal and postmenopausal women undergoing colposcopy. Subjects were further classified into ≤CIN1 and CIN2+ groups according to histopathology.
Core methods

The diagnostic and triage utility of PAX1/JAM3 methylation for identifying CIN2+ lesions were compared between premenopausal and postmenopausal women.
Main results
PAX1/JAM3 methylation demonstrated superior diagnostic performance for CIN2+ detection in postmenopausal women compared with premenopausal women, either alone or in combination. Sensitivities of PAX1m and JAM3m in postmenopausal women (90.00%, 95% CI: 76.34-97.21%; 82.50%, 95% CI: 67.22-92.66%) were higher than those in premenopausal women (61.98%, 95% CI: 52.71-70.65%; 60.33%, 95% CI: 51.04-69.11%) (all p < 0.05). The combination improved sensitivity to 72.73% in premenopausal women and maintained 92.50% in postmenopausal women. Besides, PAX1/JAM3 methylation outperformed cytology and HPV16/18 testing in both groups (p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the diagnostic performance of PAX1/JAM3 methylation for high-grade cervical lesions differs distinctly between premenopausal and postmenopausal women undergoing colposcopy. This finding highlights the need to consider menopausal status when applying methylation biomarkers.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that PAX1/JAM3 methylation could be a valuable auxiliary tool to improve diagnostic accuracy in elderly women, particularly in the context of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia detection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective, which may introduce bias and limit the generalisability of findings. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the absence of randomised controlled trials and potential confounding factors.

双功能 抗病毒药物 和抗癌肽,靶向与头颈癌相关的 人乳头瘤病毒 的复杂 宿主 病原体相互作用蛋白

A dual-functional antiviral and anticancer peptide to target intricate host-pathogen interacting proteins of human papillomavirus associated with head and neck cancer
作者： Gayathri Ashok, Soumyadip Ghosh, Sanandita Sengupta, Bikramjit Bhattacharya, Pratiksha Jana, Paul Livingstone, Sudha Ramaiah, Anand Anbarasu
摘要中文翻译头颈鳞状细胞癌在世界范围内的报道越来越多,其中一个主要因素是 发病率 感染。病毒感染激活原癌基因和信号机制,有助于肿瘤发生。阻断病毒诱发癌变的有效方法是找到一种能够有效抑制病毒和宿主蛋白的常用药物。在本研究中,检索了 HNSCC 转录组谱和参与 宿主-病原体相互作用的 HPV 基因。过表达的 HNSCC 基因被进一步考虑用于 宿主 病原体和蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用网络分析。然后将经过验证的病毒和 宿主 中心基因与具有抗癌活性的 抗病毒药物 肽对接,并进行模拟研究。相互作用组分析确定 CDK1、CCNA2、CAV1、BRCA1、TERT 和 EGFR 为 宿主 中心基因,而 E5_HPV16、E6_HPV33、E7_HPV16、18 和 33 被筛选为病毒中心基因。功能、临床、突变和生存分析筛选出 CDK1、CCNA2 和 CAV1 作为潜在的 宿主 治疗靶点。经过理化筛选,筛选出4条抗病毒药物抗癌肽,与宿主和病毒蛋白对接。其中,肽 4 (AP02569) 被认为具有较高的平均结合亲和力,对所有靶标的平均结合能为 -1048.01 kcal/mol。肽4的分子动力学模拟证明了所有靶标的对接复合物之间的整体稳定性。它进一步表明肽4在所有对接复合物中具有最小的波动,并且MM-GBSA分析表明肽4能够与所有目标蛋白形成能量稳定的复合物。我们的研究结果表明,双功能 抗病毒药物 和抗癌化合物可以成为头颈癌病毒导致的致癌作用的有效治疗干预措施。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
头颈鳞状细胞癌在世界范围内的报道越来越多,其中一个主要因素是 发病率 感染。
设计与对象
该研究检索了参与 宿主-病原体相互作用的 HNSCC 转录组谱和 HPV 基因,并进一步考虑了过表达的 HNSCC 基因用于 宿主-病原体和蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用网络分析。
核心方法
将经过验证的病毒和 宿主 中心基因与具有抗癌活性的 抗病毒药物 肽对接,并进行模拟研究。使用功能、临床、突变和生存分析来筛选潜在的 宿主 治疗靶点。
主要结果
相互作用组分析确定 CDK1、CCNA2、CAV1、BRCA1、TERT 和 EGFR 为 宿主 中心基因,而 E5_HPV16、E6_HPV33、E7_HPV16、18 和 33 被筛选为病毒中心基因。经过理化筛选,筛选出4条抗病毒药物抗癌肽,与宿主和病毒蛋白对接。肽4的分子动力学模拟证明了所有靶标的对接复合物之间的整体稳定性,MM-GBSA分析表明肽4能够与所有靶蛋白形成能量稳定的复合物。
解释与创新
该研究表明,双功能 抗病毒药物 和抗癌化合物可以成为头颈癌病毒导致的致癌作用的有效治疗干预措施。
科研与公卫意义
通过确定潜在的治疗靶点和有效的 抗病毒药物 抗癌肽,这些发现可能为 HPV 相关癌症(特别是头颈鳞状细胞癌)的靶向治疗的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有报告局限性,但证据基于模拟研究和相互作用组分析,其临床适用性可能有限。

A dual-functional antiviral and anticancer peptide to target intricate host-pathogen interacting proteins of human papillomavirus associated with head and neck cancer

Authors: Gayathri Ashok, Soumyadip Ghosh, Sanandita Sengupta, Bikramjit Bhattacharya, Pratiksha Jana, Paul Livingstone, Sudha Ramaiah, Anand Anbarasu
Original abstract (English)
Head and neck squamous cell carcinoma is increasingly being reported worldwide, and a major contributing factor is the incidence of human papillomavirus infections. The viral infection activates proto-oncogenes and signalling mechanisms aiding in tumorigenesis. An efficient way to block the virus-induced carcinogenesis is to identify a common medicament that can effectively inhibit the viral and host proteins. In the present study, HNSCC transcriptomic profiles and HPV genes involved in host-pathogen interactions were retrieved. The overexpressed HNSCC genes were further considered for host-pathogen and protein-protein interaction network analyses. Validated viral and host hub-genes were then docked with antiviral peptides with anticancer activities and subjected to simulation studies. Interactome analyses identified CDK1, CCNA2, CAV1, BRCA1, TERT and EGFR as host hub-genes, while E5_HPV16, E6_HPV33, E7_HPV16, 18 and 33 were screened as viral hub-genes. Functional, clinical, mutational and survival analyses filtered CDK1, CCNA2 and CAV1 as potential host therapeutic targets. Four antiviral-anticancer peptides were filtered after physico-chemical screening, which were docked with the host and viral proteins. Among them, peptide 4 (AP02569) was identified to have a high average binding affinity with an average binding energy of −1048.01 kcal/mol across all targets. The molecular dynamics simulations of peptide 4 demonstrated the overall stability among the docked complexes of all targets. It further indicated that peptide 4 had minimal fluctuation across all the docked complexes, and MM-GBSA analysis showed that peptide 4 was able to form energetically stable complexes with all target proteins. Our findings suggest that dual-functional antiviral and anticancer compounds can be potent therapeutic interventions for viral-led carcinogenesis in head and neck cancer.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Head and neck squamous cell carcinoma is increasingly being reported worldwide, and a major contributing factor is the incidence of human papillomavirus infections.
Design and population
The study retrieved HNSCC transcriptomic profiles and HPV genes involved in host-pathogen interactions, and further considered overexpressed HNSCC genes for host-pathogen and protein-protein interaction network analyses.
Core methods
Validated viral and host hub-genes were docked with antiviral peptides with anticancer activities and subjected to simulation studies. Functional, clinical, mutational and survival analyses were used to filter potential host therapeutic targets.
Main results
Interactome analyses identified CDK1, CCNA2, CAV1, BRCA1, TERT and EGFR as host hub-genes, while E5_HPV16, E6_HPV33, E7_HPV16, 18 and 33 were screened as viral hub-genes. Four antiviral-anticancer peptides were filtered after physico-chemical screening, which were docked with the host and viral proteins. Molecular dynamics simulations of peptide 4 demonstrated the overall stability among the docked complexes of all targets, and MM-GBSA analysis showed that peptide 4 was able to form energetically stable complexes with all target proteins.
Interpretation and novelty
The study suggests that dual-functional antiviral and anticancer compounds can be potent therapeutic interventions for viral-led carcinogenesis in head and neck cancer.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of targeted therapies for HPV-associated cancers, particularly head and neck squamous cell carcinoma, by identifying potential therapeutic targets and effective antiviral-anticancer peptides.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not report limitations, but the evidence is based on simulation studies and interactome analyses, which may have limited clinical applicability.

萨尔瓦多私人医疗保健 队列研究 中的高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 基因型

High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Genotypes in a Private Healthcare Cohort in El Salvador
作者： AARON ISAAC CORNEJO RODRIGUEZ
摘要中文翻译摘要 宫颈癌 仍然是萨尔瓦多和拉丁美洲的一个重大健康负担。虽然国家筛查计划传统上侧重于使用传统细胞学的公共卫生部门,但没有关于能够使用私人或高复杂性医疗保健系统的人群的公开文献。大多数筛查数据来自医疗保健机会有限的弱势群体。然而,评估易于进行分子检测的中高级社会经济 队列研究 使我们能够观察真正的病毒 患病率 并建立更准确的国家流行病学概况。在全球范围内,尽管 疫苗接种 做出了努力,高危基因型 (HR-HPV) 的持续存在仍是一个严峻的挑战。这是萨尔瓦多的第一项研究,采用回顾性连续抽样方案,利用分子技术对 3,245 名在三年内接受常规妇科筛查的女性(主要估计年龄在 30 至 65 岁之间)中的 14 种高风险基因型的 患病率 进行评估。通过超越传统的筛查方法,这项研究旨在为萨尔瓦多的个性化预防医学提供新的基线,包括识别可能与之前公共卫生评估中通常提供的一般信息不同的独特病毒模式。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 仍然是萨尔瓦多和拉丁美洲的重大健康负担。虽然国家筛查计划传统上侧重于使用传统细胞学的公共卫生部门,但没有关于能够使用私人或高复杂性医疗保健系统的人群的公开文献。
设计与对象
这项研究评估了萨尔瓦多的中高社会经济 队列研究,该地区易于进行分子检测,从而可以观察真正的病毒 患病率 并建立更准确的国家流行病学概况。
核心方法
这是萨尔瓦多的第一项研究,采用回顾性连续抽样方案,利用分子技术对 3,245 名在三年内接受常规妇科筛查的女性(主要估计年龄在 30 至 65 岁之间)中的 14 种高风险基因型的 患病率 进行评估。
主要结果
该研究直接观察了参与者中14种高风险基因型的患病率,提供了这些基因型在私人医疗机构中分布的具体数据。
解释与创新
这项研究通过识别可能与之前公共卫生评估中通常提供的一般信息不同的独特病毒模式,为萨尔瓦多的个性化预防医学提供了新的基线。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为私人医疗保健系统中制定更有针对性的 监测 和筛查策略提供信息,从而有可能改善 HPV 相关疾病的早期检测和预防。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对特定社会经济 队列研究 的关注而受到限制,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。由于研究的观察性质和缺乏对照组,证据的强度中等。

High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Genotypes in a Private Healthcare Cohort in El Salvador

Authors: AARON ISAAC CORNEJO RODRIGUEZ
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Cervical cancer remains a significant health burden in El Salvador and Latin America. While national screening programs have traditionally focused on public health sectors using conventional cytology, there is no published literature regarding populations with access to private or high-complexity healthcare systems. Most screening data comes from vulnerable populations with limited healthcare access. However, evaluating a middle-to-high socioeconomic cohort where molecular testing is readily available allows us to observe the true viral prevalence and build a more accurate national epidemiological profile. Globally, the persistence of high-risk genotypes (HR-HPV) represents a serious challenge despite vaccination efforts. Using a retrospective consecutive sampling scheme, this is the first study in El Salvador that uses molecular techniques to evaluate the prevalence of 14 high-risk genotypes among 3,245 women undergoing routine gynecological screening (predominantly estimated between 30 and 65 years of age) over a three-year period. By moving beyond traditional screening methods, this research aims to provide a new baseline for personalized preventive medicine in El Salvador, including the identification of unique viral patterns that may vary from general information typically available in previous public health assessments.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer remains a significant health burden in El Salvador and Latin America. While national screening programs have traditionally focused on public health sectors using conventional cytology, there is no published literature regarding populations with access to private or high-complexity healthcare systems.
Design and population
This study evaluated a middle-to-high socioeconomic cohort in El Salvador where molecular testing is readily available, allowing for the observation of true viral prevalence and the building of a more accurate national epidemiological profile.
Core methods
Using a retrospective consecutive sampling scheme, this is the first study in El Salvador that uses molecular techniques to evaluate the prevalence of 14 high-risk genotypes among 3,245 women undergoing routine gynecological screening (predominantly estimated between 30 and 65 years of age) over a three-year period.
Main results
The study directly observed the prevalence of 14 high-risk genotypes among the participants, providing specific data on the distribution of these genotypes in a private healthcare setting.
Interpretation and novelty
This research provides a new baseline for personalized preventive medicine in El Salvador by identifying unique viral patterns that may vary from general information typically available in previous public health assessments.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more targeted surveillance and screening strategies in private healthcare systems, potentially improving early detection and prevention of HPV-associated diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a specific socioeconomic cohort, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study and the lack of control groups.

便利-焦虑悖论:最低限度支持的 人乳头瘤病毒 自我采样中的癌症恐惧症和筛查误解

The Convenience-anxiety Paradox: Carcinophobia and Screening Misconceptions in Minimally Supported Human Papillomavirus Self-sampling
作者： Hatchanok Wongsuwan, Thannaporn Kittisiam, Kamolvisa Techapoonpon
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 自我采样通常涉及“最低限度支持的途径”,其中女性通过非个人渠道获得结果,无需预筛查咨询。由于医疗保健系统和患者之间的沟通真空,这种“便利-焦虑悖论”可能会导致心理困扰。本研究评估了通过阴道自我取样获得 高危型HPV 结果的女性的 患病率 和焦虑预测因子。方法 纳入了 72 名女性,其中包括医疗专业人员和大学工作人员。使用抑郁、焦虑和压力量表 21 评估焦虑程度。通过问卷收集人口统计数据、应对措施和有关 HPV 的知识。使用宫颈发育不良应激问卷评估与宫颈发育不良相关的应激。使用多元逻辑回归确定预测焦虑的因素。结果焦虑 患病率 为 23.6% ( n = 17)。在多变量分析中,健康后果子量表是一个潜在的预测因子(调整后的比值比 [aOR] = 1.60;p = 0.099)。此外,统计上不显着的趋势表明,未能区分测试的“筛查”(风险)和“诊断”(疾病)的参与者的焦虑点估计值较高(aOR = 4.37;CI = 0.94–20.36;p = 0.060)。有趣的是,从事医疗实践并不能减轻焦虑(p = 0.412),这表明在该样本中,拥有专业健康知识并不能作为对抗焦虑的保护因素。结论 HPV 自我采样的焦虑可能与筛查目的的混乱有关。由于医疗专业人员与非医务人员经历相似的焦虑水平,专业健康知识可能无法完全缓冲“信息真空”。这些结果表明存在潜在的系统性沟通差距;为了在道德上扩大自我采样的规模,通知协议应从简单的结果交付转变为健康素养敏感的干预措施,以澄清筛查的基本目的。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估通过阴道自我采样获得 患病率 结果的女性的 患病率 和焦虑预测因素,强调医疗保健系统和患者之间沟通真空造成的心理困扰。
设计与对象
该研究纳入了 72 名女性,其中包括医疗专业人员和大学工作人员,对她们进行了与 HPV 自我采样相关的焦虑程度评估。该研究是在不进行预筛查咨询的情况下通过非个人渠道提供 HPV 结果的环境中进行的。
核心方法
使用抑郁、焦虑和压力量表 21 评估焦虑程度。通过问卷收集人口统计数据、应对措施和有关 HPV 的知识。使用宫颈发育不良应激问卷评估与宫颈发育不良相关的应激。使用多元逻辑回归确定预测焦虑的因素。
主要结果
焦虑 患病率 为 23.6% (n = 17)。在多变量分析中,健康后果子量表是一个潜在的预测因子(调整后的比值比 [aOR] = 1.60;p = 0.099)。统计上不显着的趋势表明,未能区分测试的“筛查”(风险)和“诊断”(疾病)的参与者的焦虑点估计值较高(aOR = 4.37;CI = 0.94–20.36;p = 0.060)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,HPV 自我采样中的焦虑可能与筛查目的的混乱有关。这项研究强调了 HPV 检测方案中潜在的系统性沟通差距。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要采取健康素养敏感的干预措施来阐明筛查的根本目的,这可以改善自我采样计划的道德实施。
局限与证据强度
该研究的样本量较小,结果显示出不显着的趋势,表明统计功效有限。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

The Convenience-anxiety Paradox: Carcinophobia and Screening Misconceptions in Minimally Supported Human Papillomavirus Self-sampling

Authors: Hatchanok Wongsuwan, Thannaporn Kittisiam, Kamolvisa Techapoonpon
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) self-sampling often involves a “minimally supported pathway,” wherein women receive results through impersonal channels without prescreening counseling. This “convenience–anxiety paradox” may cause psychological distress due to a communication vacuum between the healthcare system and the patient. This study assessed the prevalence and predictors of anxiety among women who received high-risk HPV results through vaginal self-sampling. Methods Seventy-two women, comprising medical professionals and university staff, were included. Anxiety was evaluated using the Depression, Anxiety, and Stress Scale-21. Demographic data, coping action, and knowledge regarding HPV were collected via questionnaire. Distress related to cervical dysplasia was assessed using the Cervical Dysplasia Distress Questionnaire. Factors predicting anxiety were determined using multivariate logistic regression. Results Anxiety prevalence was 23.6% ( n = 17). In multivariate analysis, the health consequences subscale was a potential predictor (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] = 1.60; p = 0.099). Furthermore, a statistically non-significant trend indicated that participants failing to distinguish “screening” (risk) from “diagnosis” (disease) of the test had an elevated point estimate for anxiety (aOR = 4.37; CI = 0.94–20.36; p = 0.060). Interestingly, working in medical practice did not mitigate anxiety ( p = 0.412), indicating that in this sample, possessing professional health knowledge did not act as a protective factor against anxiety. Conclusions Anxiety in HPV self-sampling may be associated with confusion regarding screening’s purpose. Because medical professionals experienced similar anxiety levels as non-medical staff, professional health knowledge may not fully buffer against the “information vacuum.” These results suggest a potential systemic communication gap; to scale self-sampling ethically, notification protocols should transition from simple result delivery to health-literacy-sensitive interventions that clarify the fundamental purpose of screening.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess the prevalence and predictors of anxiety among women who received high-risk HPV results through vaginal self-sampling, highlighting the psychological distress caused by a communication vacuum between the healthcare system and the patient.
Design and population
The study included 72 women, comprising medical professionals and university staff, who were evaluated for anxiety related to HPV self-sampling. The research was conducted in a setting where HPV results were delivered through impersonal channels without prescreening counseling.
Core methods
Anxiety was evaluated using the Depression, Anxiety, and Stress Scale-21. Demographic data, coping actions, and knowledge regarding HPV were collected via questionnaire. Distress related to cervical dysplasia was assessed using the Cervical Dysplasia Distress Questionnaire. Factors predicting anxiety were determined using multivariate logistic regression.
Main results
Anxiety prevalence was 23.6% (n = 17). In multivariate analysis, the health consequences subscale was a potential predictor (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] = 1.60; p = 0.099). A statistically non-significant trend indicated that participants failing to distinguish "screening" (risk) from "diagnosis" (disease) of the test had an elevated point estimate for anxiety (aOR = 4.37; CI = 0.94–20.36; p = 0.060).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that anxiety in HPV self-sampling may be associated with confusion regarding screening’s purpose. This study highlights a potential systemic communication gap in HPV testing protocols.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that health-literacy-sensitive interventions are needed to clarify the fundamental purpose of screening, which could improve the ethical implementation of self-sampling programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's sample size was small, and the results showed a non-significant trend, indicating limited statistical power. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

宫颈淋巴细胞和外周血中 HPV16 E7 特异性 T 细胞反应与高级别宫颈上皮内瘤变病变消退的关系

Association of HPV16 E7-specific T cell responses in cervical lymphocytes and peripheral blood with regression of high-grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia lesions
作者： Kaori Nakayama-Hosoya, Kei Kawana, Saki Kamata, Naoko Tomita, Risa Yoshida, Mitsuru Toeda, Rina Kawatake, Yuki Okuma, Osamu Kobayashi, Keisuke Saito
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 虽然 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 特异性 T 细胞反应被认为有助于 HPV 相关宫颈肿瘤的消退,但其作用尚未完全阐明。我们研究了宫颈和外周血中 HPV16 E7 特异性 T 细胞反应及其与 2 级宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN2) 病理结果的关系。方法 使用 E7 重叠肽 (OLP) 池对 38 名具有明确病理结果的 HPV16 阳性 CIN2 受试者的宫颈淋巴细胞 (CxL) 和外周血单核细胞 (PBMC) 中的 HPV16 E7 特异性 T 细胞反应进行定量。使用单个 OLP 对体外扩增的 PBMC 评估这些反应的广度和 HLA 限制。结果 在超过 30% 的 CxL 样本中检测到 HPV16 E7 特异性 T 细胞反应,但在 PBMC 中很少检测到,除非在体外扩增。CxL 中 E7 刺激反应的较高强度以及非特异性背景反应的升高与 CIN2 病变的病理消退相关。扩展的 PBMC 中的 E7 特异性 T 细胞反应在受试者之间存在广度差异,并且由具有不同 HLA 限制的 CD4+ 和 CD8+ T 细胞介导。重要的是,病变消退的受试者的反应广度显着更大。结论 HPV 特异性 T 细胞反应优先位于感染部位,并在外周血中维持较低频率。CxL 中局部 E7 刺激反应的强度和扩展的 PBMC 中 E7 特异性反应的广度与 CIN2 的病理消退相关。这些关联支持 E7 特异性 T 细胞反应在 HPV 相关宫颈肿瘤消退中的潜在作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨宫颈淋巴细胞和外周血中HPV16 E7特异性T细胞反应与高级别宫颈上皮内瘤变病变(CIN2)消退之间的关系。背景强调,虽然 HPV 特异性 T 细胞反应被认为有助于 HPV 相关宫颈肿瘤的消退,但其作用仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究在 38 名患有 HPV16 阳性 CIN2 病变的受试者中进行,检查了宫颈淋巴细胞 (CxL) 和外周血单核细胞 (PBMC) 中的 T 细胞反应。该设计的重点是量化这些反应及其与确定的病理结果的关联。
核心方法
使用宫颈淋巴细胞和外周血单核细胞中的重叠肽 (OLP) 库对 HPV16 E7 特异性 T 细胞反应进行定量。使用单个 OLP 对体外扩增的 PBMC 评估这些反应的广度和 HLA 限制。分析了 CxL 中局部 E7 刺激反应的强度和扩展的 PBMC 中 E7 特异性反应的广度,以确定它们与病变消退的关联。
主要结果
在超过 30% 的 CxL 样本中检测到 HPV16 E7 特异性 T 细胞反应,但在 PBMC 中很少检测到,除非在体外扩增。CxL 中 E7 刺激反应的较高强度以及非特异性背景反应的升高与 CIN2 病变的病理消退相关。
解释与创新
作者解释说,HPV 特异性 T 细胞反应优先位于感染部位,并在外周血中维持较低的频率。这些发现表明 E7 特异性 T 细胞反应在 HPV 相关宫颈肿瘤消退中具有潜在作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为靶向免疫疗法的开发提供信息,并提高对 HPV 相关宫颈肿瘤免疫机制的了解。他们还强调了局部 免疫应答 在病变消退中的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其关注单一 HPV 类型 (HPV16) 和具有 CIN2 病变的特定人群而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为这些关联是基于观察数据而不是随机试验。

Association of HPV16 E7-specific T cell responses in cervical lymphocytes and peripheral blood with regression of high-grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia lesions

Authors: Kaori Nakayama-Hosoya, Kei Kawana, Saki Kamata, Naoko Tomita, Risa Yoshida, Mitsuru Toeda, Rina Kawatake, Yuki Okuma, Osamu Kobayashi, Keisuke Saito
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background While human papillomavirus (HPV)-specific T cell responses are believed to contribute to regression of HPV-associated cervical neoplasia, their role has not been fully elucidated. We investigated HPV16 E7-specific T cell responses in the cervix and peripheral blood and their association with pathological outcomes in cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 (CIN2). Methods HPV16 E7-specific T cell responses in cervical lymphocytes (CxLs) and peripheral blood mononuclear cells (PBMCs) were quantified using overlapping peptide (OLP) pools of E7 in 38 subjects with HPV16-positive CIN2 with defined pathological outcomes. Breadth and HLA restriction of these responses were assessed using single OLPs on in vitro-expanded PBMCs. Results HPV16 E7-specific T cell responses were detected in over 30% of CxL samples but rarely detected in PBMCs unless expanded in vitro. Higher magnitudes of E7-stimulated responses in CxLs, along with elevated non-specific background responses, were associated with pathological regression of CIN2 lesions. The E7-specific T cell responses in expanded PBMCs varied in breadth among subjects and were mediated by both CD4+ and CD8+ T cells with diverse HLA restrictions. Importantly, response breadth was significantly greater in subjects with lesion regression. Conclusions HPV-specific T cell responses are preferentially localized at the site of infection and maintained at low frequencies in peripheral blood. The magnitude of local E7-stimulated response in CxLs and the breadth of E7-specific responses in expanded PBMCs were associated with pathological regression of CIN2. These associations support a potential role for E7-specific T cell responses in the regression of HPV-related cervical neoplasia.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the association between HPV16 E7-specific T cell responses in cervical lymphocytes and peripheral blood with the regression of high-grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia lesions (CIN2). The background highlights that while HPV-specific T cell responses are believed to contribute to regression of HPV-associated cervical neoplasia, their role remains unclear.
Design and population
The study was conducted in 38 subjects with HPV16-positive CIN2 lesions, examining T cell responses in cervical lymphocytes (CxLs) and peripheral blood mononuclear cells (PBMCs). The design focused on quantifying these responses and their association with defined pathological outcomes.
Core methods
HPV16 E7-specific T cell responses were quantified using overlapping peptide (OLP) pools in cervical lymphocytes and peripheral blood mononuclear cells. The breadth and HLA restriction of these responses were assessed using single OLPs on in vitro-expanded PBMCs. The magnitude of local E7-stimulated responses in CxLs and the breadth of E7-specific responses in expanded PBMCs were analyzed for their association with lesion regression.
Main results
HPV16 E7-specific T cell responses were detected in over 30% of CxL samples but were rarely detected in PBMCs unless expanded in vitro. Higher magnitudes of E7-stimulated responses in CxLs, along with elevated non-specific background responses, were associated with pathological regression of CIN2 lesions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that HPV-specific T cell responses are preferentially localized at the site of infection and maintained at low frequencies in peripheral blood. These findings suggest a potential role for E7-specific T cell responses in the regression of HPV-related cervical neoplasia.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of targeted immunotherapies and improve understanding of immune mechanisms in HPV-related cervical neoplasia. They also highlight the importance of local immune responses in lesion regression.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single HPV type (HPV16) and a specific population with CIN2 lesions. The evidence is moderate, as the associations are based on observational data and not on randomized trials.

患病率 和访问阿姆斯特丹性健康中心的男男性行为未接种疫苗的年轻男性肛门高危 人乳头瘤病毒 感染的相关危险因素

Prevalence and associated risk factors of anal high-risk human papillomavirus infection in unvaccinated young men who have sex with men visiting the Center of Sexual Health of Amsterdam
作者： Sarah van Veelen, Catharina J. Alberts, Nina Barendsen, Lobke Massop, Elske Hoornenborg, Sylvia Maria Bruisten, Johannes A. Bogaards, Maarten Schim van der Loeff
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估在阿姆斯特丹性健康中心 (CSH) 就读的未接种疫苗的男男性行为者 (MSM) 的 患病率 和肛门高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 的决定因素。这很重要,因为 HPV 疫苗接种 在该人群中的有效性高度依赖于之前的 HPV 暴露。
设计与对象
该研究纳入了 2023-2024 年全国 HPV 补种运动期间访问阿姆斯特丹 CSH 的 431 名 19-28 岁未接种疫苗的 MSM。参与者提供了社会人口统计和性行为信息、静脉血样本和肛门自我样本。
核心方法
对样本进行了 HPV、HIV、衣原体、淋病和梅毒检测。使用 SPF10 DEIA/LiPA-25 系统对 HPV DNA 阳性肛门样本进行基因分型。使用多变量逻辑回归和广义估计方程检查 hrHPV 阳性和 HPV16/18 的决定因素。
主要结果
在 431 名 MSM 中,51.0% 的任何肛门 hrHPV 型检测呈阳性,14.6% 的 HPV16 检测呈阳性,8.1% 的 HPV18 检测呈阳性。在多变量分析中,终生性伴侣数量、接受性肛交期间安全套使用不一致以及肛门淋病与 hrHPV 阳性相关。
解释与创新
研究发现,年轻未接种疫苗的 MSM 中 hrHPV 的 患病率 较高,与性行为和不一致的安全套使用有关。这些发现将作为分析 HPV 疫苗接种 在荷兰 MSM 中的预防效果和人群影响的基础。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以让 监测、诊断 和 治疗 了解年轻 MSM 中的 hrHPV,并强调性健康教育和持续使用安全套对于减少 hrHPV 感染和相关风险的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系。该样本来自荷兰的一个中心,这可能会限制普遍性。该研究具有很高的置信度。

Prevalence and associated risk factors of anal high-risk human papillomavirus infection in unvaccinated young men who have sex with men visiting the Center of Sexual Health of Amsterdam

Authors: Sarah van Veelen, Catharina J. Alberts, Nina Barendsen, Lobke Massop, Elske Hoornenborg, Sylvia Maria Bruisten, Johannes A. Bogaards, Maarten Schim van der Loeff
Original abstract (English)
Background: Anal high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) is highly prevalent among men who have sex with men (MSM) and the main cause of anal cancer. The effectiveness of HPV vaccination in this population is highly dependent on prior HPV exposure. We aimed to assess the prevalence and determinants of anal hrHPV among young unvaccinated MSM attending the Center for Sexual Health (CSH) of Amsterdam. These findings will serve as a basis for analyses regarding the prophylactic effectiveness and population impact of HPV vaccination among MSM in the Netherlands. Methods: All unvaccinated MSM aged 19–28 years visiting the CSH of Amsterdam during the National 2023-2024 HPV catch-up campaign were eligible. Participants provided socio-demographic and sexual behaviour information, a venous blood sample and anal self-sample. Samples were tested for HPV, HIV, chlamydia, gonorrhoea, and syphilis. HPV DNA-positive anal samples were genotyped using the SPF10 DEIA/LiPA-25 system. Determinants of hrHPV positivity and HPV16/18 were examined using multivariable logistic regression with generalized estimating equations. Results: Among 431 MSM (median age 24 years), 51.0% tested positive for any anal hrHPV-type, 14.6% for HPV16 and 8.1% for HPV18. In multivariable analyses, lifetime number of sexual partners (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] per natural log increase: 1.33, 95%CI 1.16–1.51), inconsistent versus consistent condom use during receptive anal intercourse (aOR 1.68, 95%CI 1.02–2.76) and anal gonorrhoea (aOR 1.51, 95%CI 1.00-2.29) were associated with hrHPV positivity. Conclusions: Prevalence of hrHPV among young unvaccinated MSM is high and associated with sexual behaviours and inconsistent condom use.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the prevalence and determinants of anal high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) among young unvaccinated men who have sex with men (MSM) attending the Center for Sexual Health (CSH) of Amsterdam. This is important because the effectiveness of HPV vaccination in this population is highly dependent on prior HPV exposure.
Design and population
The study included 431 unvaccinated MSM aged 19–28 years visiting the CSH of Amsterdam during the National 2023-2024 HPV catch-up campaign. Participants provided socio-demographic and sexual behavior information, a venous blood sample, and an anal self-sample.
Core methods
Samples were tested for HPV, HIV, chlamydia, gonorrhoea, and syphilis. HPV DNA-positive anal samples were genotyped using the SPF10 DEIA/LiPA-25 system. Determinants of hrHPV positivity and HPV16/18 were examined using multivariable logistic regression with generalized estimating equations.
Main results
Among 431 MSM, 51.0% tested positive for any anal hrHPV-type, 14.6% for HPV16, and 8.1% for HPV18. In multivariable analyses, lifetime number of sexual partners, inconsistent condom use during receptive anal intercourse, and anal gonorrhoea were associated with hrHPV positivity.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found a high prevalence of hrHPV among young unvaccinated MSM, with associations with sexual behaviors and inconsistent condom use. These findings will serve as a basis for analyses regarding the prophylactic effectiveness and population impact of HPV vaccination among MSM in the Netherlands.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of hrHPV among young MSM, and highlight the importance of sexual health education and consistent condom use to reduce hrHPV infection and associated risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality. The sample was from a single center in the Netherlands, which may limit generalizability. The study has a high confidence level.

微核细胞分析揭示 高危型HPV 阳性女性宫颈癌前病变的基因组不稳定性

Micronucleus cytome assay reveals genomic instability in premalignant cervical lesions among high-risk HPV-positive women
作者： Nohelia Cajas-Salazar, Leidy J. Mueses-Moreno, Carlos H. Sierra-Torres
摘要中文翻译宫颈癌 是高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 持续感染造成的主要健康负担。微核细胞组 (MNcyt) 检测中的基因组不稳定性生物标志物可以改善对细胞学和 HPV 检测之外的进展风险女性的识别。在哥伦比亚波帕扬对 184 名参加常规 宫颈癌 筛查的女性进行了一项横断面研究。收集脱落的宫颈上皮细胞,使用 Feulgen-Fast Green DNA 特异性方法进行 HPV DNA 基因分型、液基细胞学和 MNcyt 分析。对于每个样品,对 2,000 个细胞的微核、核芽、双核细胞、固缩细胞、核分裂细胞、核溶解细胞和具有浓缩染色质的细胞进行评分。跨有序 HPV/细胞学类别评估组间差异和趋势;基因型分析使用年龄调整模型,并且在 HPV 阳性女性中,使用年龄和细胞学调整模型。女性被分类为 HPV 阴性,上皮内病变或恶性肿瘤阴性(HPV-/NILM,n = 69),HPV 阳性 NILM(HPV+/NILM,n = 47),HPV 阳性,伴有意义不明的非典型鳞状细胞或低度鳞状上皮内病变(HPV+/ASCUS-LSIL,n = 49),以及 HPV 阳性,伴有高级鳞状上皮内病变病变或更严重(HPV+/≥HSIL,n = 19)。在这些有序类别中,观察到微核 (p = 0.044)、核芽 (p = 0.029) 和双核细胞 (p = 0.011) 的显着正趋势。在 HPV 阳性女性中,根据年龄和细胞学调整后,观察到核芽 (p = 0.046)、核溶解细胞 (p = 0.010) 和浓缩染色质 (p = 0.035) 的基因型差异。总体而言,微核、核芽和双核细胞在 HPV/细胞学类别中表现出一致的基因组不稳定性模式,而以基因型为重点的分析也发现了选定的基因毒性和细胞毒性终点的差异。这些发现支持 MNcyt 生物标志物作为 HPV 相关宫颈疾病风险评估的补充方法的潜在价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 是高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 持续感染造成的主要健康负担。
设计与对象
在哥伦比亚波帕扬对 184 名参加常规 宫颈癌 筛查的女性进行了一项横断面研究。
核心方法
微核细胞组 (MNcyt) 检测中的基因组不稳定性生物标志物可以改善对细胞学和 HPV 检测之外的进展风险女性的识别。
主要结果
在这些有序类别中,观察到微核 (p = 0.044)、核芽 (p = 0.029) 和双核细胞 (p = 0.011) 的显着正趋势。
解释与创新
总体而言,微核、核芽和双核细胞在 HPV/细胞学类别中表现出一致的基因组不稳定性模式,而以基因型为重点的分析也发现了选定的基因毒性和细胞毒性终点的差异。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持 MNcyt 生物标志物作为 HPV 相关宫颈疾病风险评估的补充方法的潜在价值。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Micronucleus cytome assay reveals genomic instability in premalignant cervical lesions among high-risk HPV-positive women

Authors: Nohelia Cajas-Salazar, Leidy J. Mueses-Moreno, Carlos H. Sierra-Torres
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer is a major health burden driven by persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV). Genomic instability biomarkers from the micronucleus cytome (MNcyt) assay may improve identification of women at risk of progression beyond cytology and HPV testing. A cross-sectional study of 184 women attending routine cervical cancer screening was conducted in Popayán, Colombia. Exfoliated cervical epithelial cells were collected for HPV DNA genotyping, liquid-based cytology, and MNcyt analysis using the Feulgen-Fast Green DNA-specific method. For each sample, 2,000 cells were scored for micronuclei, nuclear buds, binucleated cells, pyknotic cells, karyorrhectic cells, karyolytic cells, and cells with condensed chromatin. Group differences and trends were assessed across ordered HPV/cytology categories; genotype analyses used age-adjusted models and, among HPV-positive women, models adjusted for age and cytology. Women were classified as HPV-negative with negative for intraepithelial lesion or malignancy (HPV-/NILM, n = 69), HPV-positive with NILM (HPV+/NILM, n = 47), HPV-positive with atypical squamous cells of undetermined significance or low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (HPV+/ASCUS-LSIL, n = 49), and HPV-positive with high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion or worse (HPV+/≥HSIL, n = 19). Significant positive trends across these ordered categories were observed for micronuclei (p = 0.044), nuclear buds (p = 0.029), and binucleated cells (p = 0.011). Among HPV-positive women, genotype differences adjusted for age and cytology were observed for nuclear buds (p = 0.046), karyolytic cells (p = 0.010), and condensed chromatin (p = 0.035). Overall, micronuclei, nuclear buds, and binucleated cells showed consistent patterns of genomic instability across HPV/cytology categories, while genotype-focused analyses also identified differences in selected genotoxic and cytotoxic endpoints. These findings support the potential value of MNcyt biomarkers as a complementary approach for risk assessment in HPV-related cervical disease.

Design and population

Core methods

Main results
Significant positive trends across these ordered categories were observed for micronuclei (p = 0.044), nuclear buds (p = 0.029), and binucleated cells (p = 0.011).
Interpretation and novelty

肯尼亚女性宫颈阴道微生物群与 HPV 感染和疾病相关的生态和推断功能景观

Ecological and inferred functional landscape of the cervicovaginal microbiome in relation to HPV infection and disease in Kenyan women
作者： Mariano A. Molina, Kosgei Noah, Ivy Akinyi, Lisse Tavernier, Sadaf Sakina Hassan, Milan S. Stosic, Davy Vanden Broeck, Hillary Mabeya, Laila Sara Arroyo Mühr
摘要中文翻译摘要 持续高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是 宫颈癌 的主要原因,但微生物组与从正常细胞学到宫颈病变的疾病严重程度之间的关系仍不完全清楚,特别是在非洲人群中。在这里,我们使用 16S rRNA 基因测序、HPV 基因分型和阴道毛滴虫检测对 91 名细胞学阴性、低度或重度鳞状上皮内病变的肯尼亚女性的宫颈阴道微生物组进行了表征。宫颈阴道群落分为六种不同的生态状态或宫颈类型,其主要分类群和组成不同。病变严重程度的增加与乳酸菌群落的消耗和特定细菌类群的变化有关。微生物共现分析进一步鉴定了乳杆菌、加德纳菌和代尔夫特菌相关的生态模块,其中乳杆菌相关的模块与细胞学严重程度和HPV感染呈负相关。推断的功能分析确定了 35 条与病变严重程度增加显着相关的途径,包括碳水化合物、氨基酸、脂肪酸、芳香族化合物、核苷酸和辅因子代谢的变化。这些推断的功能特征与 Delftia 相关模块相关。探索性惩罚回归分析表明,除了临床变量之外,生态特征还提供了适度的歧视性信息。总而言之,这些发现确定了与宫颈疾病严重程度相关的宫颈阴道微生物组的协调生态和推断功能变化,并支持进一步研究 HPV 相关宫颈疾病的微生物生态组织。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解肯尼亚女性的宫颈阴道微生物群与宫颈疾病严重程度之间的关系,特别是与 HPV 感染相关的关系,从而解决非洲人群中微生物群与 宫颈癌 之间尚未完全了解的联系。
设计与对象
该研究使用 16S rRNA 基因测序、HPV 基因分型和阴道毛滴虫检测,对 2025 年至 2026 年 91 名细胞学阴性、低度或重度鳞状上皮内病变的肯尼亚女性的宫颈阴道微生物组进行了表征。
核心方法
宫颈阴道群落被分为六种不同的生态状态或宫颈类型,微生物共现分析确定了乳杆菌、加德纳菌和代尔夫特菌相关的生态模块。功能分析揭示了 35 条与病变严重程度增加显着相关的通路,与 Delftia 相关模块相关。
主要结果
病变严重程度的增加与乳酸菌群落的消耗和特定细菌类群的变化有关。微生物共现分析进一步鉴定了乳杆菌、加德纳菌和代尔夫特菌相关的生态模块,其中乳杆菌相关的模块与细胞学严重程度和 HPV 感染呈负相关。功能分析确定了 35 条与病变严重程度增加显着相关的途径,包括与 Delftia 相关模块相关的碳水化合物、氨基酸、脂肪酸、芳香化合物、核苷酸和辅因子代谢的变化。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,宫颈阴道微生物组的生态和推断功能变化与宫颈疾病严重程度相关,并支持进一步研究 HPV 相关宫颈疾病的微生物生态组织。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过识别潜在的生物标志物和功能途径,为 监测、诊断 和 治疗 提供有关 HPV 相关宫颈疾病的信息。
局限与证据强度
除了临床变量之外,该研究的生态特征还提供了适度的歧视性信息,样本量和特定的肯尼亚人群可能会限制普遍性。

Ecological and inferred functional landscape of the cervicovaginal microbiome in relation to HPV infection and disease in Kenyan women

Authors: Mariano A. Molina, Kosgei Noah, Ivy Akinyi, Lisse Tavernier, Sadaf Sakina Hassan, Milan S. Stosic, Davy Vanden Broeck, Hillary Mabeya, Laila Sara Arroyo Mühr
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is the central cause of cervical cancer, but the association of the microbiome with disease severity from normal cytology to cervical lesions remains incompletely understood, particularly in African populations. Here, we characterized the cervicovaginal microbiome of 91 Kenyan women with negative cytology, low-grade, or high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions using 16S rRNA gene sequencing, HPV genotyping, and Trichomonas vaginalis detection. Cervicovaginal communities resolved into six distinct ecological states or cervicotypes that differed in dominant taxa and composition. Increasing lesion severity was associated with depletion of Lactobacillus communities and shifts in specific bacterial taxa. Microbial co-occurrence analysis further identified Lactobacillus -, Gardnerella -, and Delftia -associated ecological modules, with the Lactobacillus -associated module inversely related to cytological severity and HPV infection. Inferred functional profiling identified 35 pathways significantly associated with increasing lesion severity, including changes in carbohydrate, amino acid, fatty acid, aromatic compound, nucleotide, and cofactor metabolism. These inferred functional signatures correlated with the Delftia -associated module. Exploratory penalized regression analyses showed that ecological features provided modest discriminatory information in addition to clinical variables. Altogether, these findings identify coordinated ecological and inferred functional changes in the cervicovaginal microbiome associated with cervical disease severity and support further investigation of microbial ecological organization in HPV-associated cervical disease.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the association between the cervicovaginal microbiome and cervical disease severity, particularly in relation to HPV infection, in Kenyan women, addressing the incompletely understood link between the microbiome and cervical cancer in African populations.
Design and population
The study characterized the cervicovaginal microbiome of 91 Kenyan women with negative cytology, low-grade, or high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions using 16S rRNA gene sequencing, HPV genotyping, and Trichomonas vaginalis detection, spanning from 2025 to 2026.
Core methods
Cervicovaginal communities were resolved into six distinct ecological states or cervicotypes, and microbial co-occurrence analysis identified Lactobacillus-, Gardnerella-, and Delftia-associated ecological modules. Functional profiling revealed 35 pathways significantly associated with increasing lesion severity, correlated with the Delftia-associated module.
Main results
Increasing lesion severity was associated with depletion of Lactobacillus communities and shifts in specific bacterial taxa. Microbial co-occurrence analysis further identified Lactobacillus-, Gardnerella-, and Delftia-associated ecological modules, with the Lactobacillus-associated module inversely related to cytological severity and HPV infection. Functional profiling identified 35 pathways significantly associated with increasing lesion severity, including changes in carbohydrate, amino acid, fatty acid, aromatic compound, nucleotide, and cofactor metabolism, correlated with the Delftia-associated module.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest coordinated ecological and inferred functional changes in the cervicovaginal microbiome associated with cervical disease severity and support further investigation of microbial ecological organization in HPV-associated cervical disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings could inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of HPV-associated cervical disease by identifying potential biomarkers and functional pathways.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's ecological features provided modest discriminatory information in addition to clinical variables, and the sample size and specific Kenyan population may limit generalizability.
