四价 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 对 HIV 女性的长期免疫原性和功效:一项纵向 队列研究 研究。

Long-term immunogenicity and efficacy of the quadrivalent human papillomavirus vaccine in women with HIV: a longitudinal cohort study.
作者： Elisabeth McClymont, Sandra Blitz, Sofia Nicolls, Marette Lee, François Coutlée, Sharon Walmsley, Sylvie Trottier, Fiona Smaill, Mona Loutfy, Mark H Yudin
摘要中文翻译背景:了解人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗在HIV感染女性中的影响对于实现HPV消除至关重要,因为HIV感染女性HPV感染率和相关疾病发生率较高。我们旨在评估四价HPV疫苗(qHPV)在HIV感染女性队列中接种至9年后的免疫原性和有效性。方法:这项纵向多中心队列研究招募了来自加拿大14家提供HIV和妇科护理诊所的年龄在9岁及以上的HIV感染女孩和女性。参与者在0、2和6个月时接受三剂qHPV疫苗,并从2008年7月1日至2019年6月27日进行随访。在基线和每6-12个月进行一次临床数据收集、抗HPV血清学检测、液基宫颈细胞学检查和HPV DNA分型。免疫原性终点包括抗HPV几何平均滴度(GMTs)和血清阳性率。我们使用扩展Cox回归模型评估了随时间变化的HIV参数对血清转阴时间的影响。对于年龄受限的子集(24-45岁),抗HPV GMTs与已发表的队列研究进行了比较,采用单样本t检验。有效性终点包括持续性qHPV的发病率、生殖器疣的发病率以及宫颈上皮内瘤变2级及以上(CIN2+)的发病率。结果:在符合方案的人群中,共有228名参与者,基线时中位年龄为38·5岁(IQR 32·0-45·4),中位CD4计数为499·5(IQR 380·8-700·0)个/μL,其中160名(70·2%)参与者HIV病毒载量被抑制(<50拷贝/毫升)。中位随访时间为5·9年(IQR 2·3-7·7)。在符合方案的人群中,接种后第7个月出现非常高的血清转化率,随访期间HPV6的血清转阴率为132例中的18例(13·6%),HPV11的血清转阴率为186例中的22例(11·8%),HPV16的血清转阴率为141例中的12例(8·5%),HPV18的血清转阴率为165例中的60例(36·4%)。峰值抗体滴度和连续HIV病毒载量是血清转阴时间的重要预测因素。在年龄受限的子集(24-45岁)中,抗HPV GMTs在HIV感染女性和非HIV感染女性之间在9年内相似。在符合方案的人群中,持续性qHPV的发病率是0·52(95% CI 0·22-1·10)每100人年,生殖器疣的发病率是0·37(0·12-0·87)每100人年,CIN2+的发病率是0·00(0·00-0·24)每100人年。解释:这些数据显示,接种疫苗的HIV感染女性中感染和疾病的发生率较低。接种疫苗后持续的HIV病毒载量抑制对于确保持续保护至关重要。资助:加拿大卫生研究院(CIHR)、CIHR加拿大HIV试验网络以及默克加拿大公司的实物贡献。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
了解 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 对 HIV 感染女性(HPV 感染及相关疾病发生率较高)的影响对于实现 宫颈癌 消除至关重要。
设计与对象
这项纵向多中心 队列研究 研究从加拿大各地提供艾滋病毒和妇科护理的 14 家诊所招募了 9 岁及以上感染艾滋病毒的女孩和妇女。参与者在0、2和6个月时接受了三剂qHPV 疫苗,并于2008年7月1日至2019年6月27日进行随访。
核心方法
在基线和每 6-12 个月进行一次临床数据、抗 HPV 血清学、液基宫颈细胞学检查和 HPV DNA 基因分型。免疫原性终点是抗 HPV 几何平均滴度 (GMT) 和血清阳性率。该研究利用扩展的 Cox 回归模型评估了随时间变化的 HIV 参数对血清逆转时间的影响。
主要结果
在符合方案的人群中,第 7 个月的血清转换率非常高,在随访期间,132 例 HPV6 血清中有 18 例 (13·6%),186 例 HPV11 中有 22 例 (11·8%),141 例 HPV16 中有 12 例 (8·5%),165 例 HPV18 中有 60 例 (36·4%)。峰值抗体滴度和连续 HIV 病毒载量是血清逆转时间的重要预测因素。在长达 9 年的时间里,感染和未感染 HIV 的女性(历史比较组)的抗 HPV GMT 相似。
解释与创新
这些数据显示,接种 队列研究 的 HIV 女性感染率和患病率较低。该研究为四价 HPV 疫苗 对长期感染 HIV 的女性的免疫原性和功效提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
疫苗接种 后持续抑制 HIV 病毒载量对于确保持续保护非常重要。这些发现对于感染艾滋病毒的女性中 HPV 相关疾病的管理具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其对历史比较组的依赖以及与艾滋病毒管理相关的混杂因素的可能性。由于研究的观察性质和比较组的使用,证据的强度中等。

Long-term immunogenicity and efficacy of the quadrivalent human papillomavirus vaccine in women with HIV: a longitudinal cohort study.

Authors: Elisabeth McClymont, Sandra Blitz, Sofia Nicolls, Marette Lee, François Coutlée, Sharon Walmsley, Sylvie Trottier, Fiona Smaill, Mona Loutfy, Mark H Yudin
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Understanding the impact of the human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine in women with HIV, who have higher rates of HPV infection and associated disease, is vital to achieving cervical cancer elimination. We aimed to assess the immunogenicity and efficacy of the quadrivalent HPV (qHPV) vaccine up to 9 years in a cohort of women with HIV. METHODS: This longitudinal multicentre cohort study recruited girls and women with HIV aged 9 years and older from 14 clinics providing HIV and gynaecological care across Canada. Participants were administered three doses of qHPV vaccine at 0, 2, and 6 months and followed up from July 1, 2008, to June 27, 2019. Clinical data, anti-HPV serology, liquid-based cervical cytology, and HPV DNA genotyping were conducted at baseline and every 6-12 months. Immunogenicity endpoints were anti-HPV geometric mean titres (GMTs) and seropositivity rates. We assessed the effect of time-varying HIV parameters on time to seroreversion with extended Cox regression models. GMTs for an age-restricted subset (24-45 years) were compared with a published cohort with one-sample t tests. Efficacy endpoints were the incidence of persistent qHPV, genital warts, and cervical intraepithelial neoplasia of grade two and higher (CIN2+). FINDINGS: There were 228 participants in the per-protocol population, with a median age at baseline of 38·5 years (IQR 32·0-45·4), median CD4 count of 499·5 (IQR 380·8-700·0) cells per μL, and 160 (70·2%) participants had suppressed HIV viral loads (<50 copies per mL). Median follow-up was 5·9 years (IQR 2·3-7·7). In the per-protocol population, following very high seroconversion rates at month 7, during follow-up there were 18 (13·6%) of 132 seroreversions for HPV6, 22 (11·8%) of 186 for HPV11, 12 (8·5%) of 141 for HPV16, and 60 (36·4%) of 165 for HPV18. Peak antibody titres and serial HIV viral load were significant predictors of time to seroreversion. Anti-HPV GMTs were similar between women with and without HIV (historical comparator group) for up to 9 years. In the per-protocol population, the incidence of persistent qHPV was 0·52 (95% CI 0·22-1·10) per 100 person-years, the incidence of genital warts was 0·37 (0·12-0·87) per 100 person-years, and the incidence of CIN2+ was 0·00 (0·00-0·24) per 100 person-years. INTERPRETATION: These data show low rates of infection and disease in this vaccinated cohort of women with HIV. Continual HIV viral load suppression after vaccination is important to ensure continued protection. FUNDING: Canadian Institutes for Health Research (CIHR), CIHR Canadian HIV Trials Network, and in-kind contribution from Merck Canada.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Understanding the impact of the human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine in women with HIV, who have higher rates of HPV infection and associated disease, is vital to achieving cervical cancer elimination.
Design and population
This longitudinal multicentre cohort study recruited girls and women with HIV aged 9 years and older from 14 clinics providing HIV and gynaecological care across Canada. Participants were administered three doses of qHPV vaccine at 0, 2, and 6 months and followed up from July 1, 2008, to June 27, 2019.
Core methods
Clinical data, anti-HPV serology, liquid-based cervical cytology, and HPV DNA genotyping were conducted at baseline and every 6-12 months. Immunogenicity endpoints were anti-HPV geometric mean titres (GMTs) and seropositivity rates. The study assessed the effect of time-varying HIV parameters on time to seroreversion with extended Cox regression models.
Main results
In the per-protocol population, following very high seroconversion rates at month 7, during follow-up there were 18 (13·6%) of 132 seroreversions for HPV6, 22 (11·8%) of 186 for HPV11, 12 (8·5%) of 141 for HPV16, and 60 (36·4%) of 165 for HPV18. Peak antibody titres and serial HIV viral load were significant predictors of time to seroreversion. Anti-HPV GMTs were similar between women with and without HIV (historical comparator group) for up to 9 years.
Interpretation and novelty
These data show low rates of infection and disease in this vaccinated cohort of women with HIV. The study provides novel insights into the immunogenicity and efficacy of the quadrivalent HPV vaccine in women with HIV over a long-term period.
Scientific and public-health significance
Continual HIV viral load suppression after vaccination is important to ensure continued protection. The findings have implications for the management of HPV-related diseases in women with HIV.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a historical comparator group and the potential for confounding factors related to HIV management. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study and the use of a comparator group.

Journal: Infectious Agents and Cancer  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-05  |  DOI: 10.1186/s13027-026-00805-z  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

HPV 相关 宫颈癌 中的 β-葡聚糖:当前证据和转化挑战

β-glucans in HPV-Associated cervical cancer: current evidence and translational challenges
作者： Mahdieh Ameri Shah Reza, Asrin Ahmadi, Hamed Afkhami, Behrouz Akbari-Adergani, Saeed Reza Pakzad, Shahram Shoeibi, Mohamad Mahjoor, Alireza Shadab, Behrouz Akbari‐adergani
摘要中文翻译尽管筛查和 HPV 疫苗接种 取得了进展,但 宫颈癌 仍然是全世界癌症相关发病率和 死亡率 的主要原因。当前治疗方法的局限性,包括毒性、耐药性和晚期疾病的次优疗效,激发了人们对补充免疫调节策略的兴趣。β-葡聚糖是源自真菌、酵母、细菌、谷物和藻类的天然多糖,因其免疫调节和药物输送能力而受到越来越多的关注。主要来自非宫颈肿瘤模型的临床前证据表明,β-葡聚糖可以通过 Dectin-1、补体受体 3 (CR3) 和 Toll 样受体等受体调节先天性和适应性免疫,可能重塑肿瘤微环境并增强抗肿瘤反应。宫颈癌 的直接证据仍然相对有限,但正在出现:一项小型随机对照试验最近证明,局部 β-葡聚糖治疗可改善 HPV 清除率,1980 年代至 1990 年代的历史试验报道了作为侵入性 宫颈癌 放疗佐剂的益处,同时在体外排列的 宫颈癌 细胞中观察到直接细胞毒性活性。与此同时,基于 β-葡聚糖的递送系统已显示出改善抗癌药物的稳定性、靶向性和控制释放的潜力,尽管这些平台同样主要在非宫颈模型中得到验证。这篇综述严格地区分了宫颈特异性证据与从其他恶性肿瘤推断的发现,确定了关键证据差距,包括缺乏以 HPV 为重点的机制研究和标准化配方,并概述了未来在 宫颈癌 治疗中将 β-葡聚糖临床转化的研究重点。

问题与背景
尽管筛查和 HPV 疫苗接种 取得了进展,但 宫颈癌 仍然是全世界癌症相关发病率和 死亡率 的主要原因。
设计与对象
当前治疗方法的局限性,包括毒性、耐药性和晚期疾病的次优疗效,激发了人们对补充免疫调节策略的兴趣。
核心方法
β-葡聚糖是源自真菌、酵母、细菌、谷物和藻类的天然多糖,因其免疫调节和药物输送能力而受到越来越多的关注。
主要结果
宫颈癌 的直接证据仍然相对有限,但正在不断出现:一项小型随机对照试验最近证明,局部 β-葡聚糖治疗可改善 HPV 清除率,1980 年代至 1990 年代的历史试验报告了作为侵入性 宫颈癌 放疗佐剂的益处,同时在体外 宫颈癌 细胞系中观察到直接细胞毒性活性。
解释与创新
主要来自非宫颈肿瘤模型的临床前证据表明,β-葡聚糖可以通过 Dectin-1、补体受体 3 (CR3) 和 Toll 样受体等受体调节先天性和适应性免疫,可能重塑肿瘤微环境并增强抗肿瘤反应。
科研与公卫意义
这篇综述严格地区分了宫颈特异性证据与从其他恶性肿瘤推断的发现,确定了关键证据差距,包括缺乏以 HPV 为重点的机制研究和标准化配方,并概述了未来在 宫颈癌 治疗中将 β-葡聚糖临床转化的研究重点。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据没有报告与研究设计或样本量相关的具体限制。

β-glucans in HPV-Associated cervical cancer: current evidence and translational challenges

Authors: Mahdieh Ameri Shah Reza, Asrin Ahmadi, Hamed Afkhami, Behrouz Akbari-Adergani, Saeed Reza Pakzad, Shahram Shoeibi, Mohamad Mahjoor, Alireza Shadab, Behrouz Akbari‐adergani
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer continues to be a major cause of cancer-related morbidity and mortality worldwide despite advances in screening and HPV vaccination. Limitations of current therapeutic approaches, including toxicity, resistance, and suboptimal efficacy in advanced disease, have stimulated interest in complementary immunomodulatory strategies. β-glucans, naturally occurring polysaccharides derived from fungi, yeast, bacteria, cereals, and algae, have attracted increasing attention for their immunoregulatory and drug-delivery capabilities. Preclinical evidence, largely derived from non-cervical tumor models, indicates that β-glucans can modulate innate and adaptive immunity through receptors such as Dectin-1, complement receptor 3 (CR3), and Toll-like receptors, potentially remodeling the tumor microenvironment and enhancing antitumor responses. Direct evidence in cervical cancer remains comparatively limited but is emerging: a small randomized controlled trial recently demonstrated improved HPV clearance with topical β-glucans therapy, and historical trials from the 1980s-1990s reported benefit as a radiotherapy adjuvant in invasive cervical cancer, alongside direct cytotoxic activity observed in cervical cancer cell lined in vitro. In parallel, β-glucans-based delivery systems have shown potential for improving the stability, targeting, and controlled release of anticancer agents, though these platforms have likewise been validated primarily in non-cervical models. This review critically distinguishes cervical-specific evidence from findings extrapolated from other malignancies, identifies key evidence gap, including the scarcity of HPV-focused mechanistic studies and standardized formulations, and outlines priorities for future research toward the clinical translation of β-glucans in cervical cancer management.

Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations related to the study design or sample size.

叙利亚女性对 宫颈癌、人乳头瘤病毒、疫苗接种 和 宫颈癌 筛查的认识:一项横断面研究

Awareness of cervical cancer, human papillomavirus vaccination, and cervical cancer screening among syrian women: a cross-sectional study
作者： Ahmad Nabil Alhouri, Rami Akwan, Farah Asif Sahloul, Dania Taisir Keshkeh, Fatima Alhouri, Abir Koudsi
摘要中文翻译尽管 宫颈癌 是高度可预防的,但它仍然是一个主要的公共卫生问题,特别是在叙利亚等资源匮乏的地区。由于国家数据有限,我们旨在评估叙利亚妇女对 宫颈癌 及其预防策略的认识,以及她们对这些方法的实践。对居住在大马士革和大马士革农村地区 16 岁或以上的叙利亚妇女进行了一项横断面研究,使用了一份改编自 CCKP-64 工具的匿名自填问卷,并使用滚雪球抽样技术,于 2025 年 12 月 1 日至 2026 年 3 月 1 日通过社交媒体平台在线分发。使用 IBM SPSS Statistics v25 进行数据分析。数据收集自 405 名叙利亚妇女。观察到知识与行动之间存在差距。尽管大多数参与者认识到 宫颈癌 可能与感染有关 (86.2%),并且对子宫颈抹片检查有一定了解 (59.8%),但实际筛查率很低,只有 6.9% 的人进行过筛查。HPV 疫苗 的认知度有限(35.1%),接种疫苗的比例更小(3%),只有 11.6% 的人正确识别出 疫苗接种 的最佳年龄范围为 9 至 13 岁。值得注意的是,只有一小部分参与者(9.1%)与受影响的个人有直接接触,31.9%的人认为这种疾病将来可能会影响他们。对风险因素的了解程度中等(平均分:49.16 ± 8.14(满分 85 分)),但对于尚未确定的风险因素仍然存在误解。多变量分析显示,父母医疗保健就业和 16-19 岁以上年龄组独立预测对风险因素的更高了解(p < 0.05)。叙利亚妇女对 宫颈癌 的高度认识并未转化为预防行动,这证实仅靠知识是不够的;因此,迫切需要将 HPV 疫苗接种 纳入国家免疫规划以及有针对性的教育工作。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管 宫颈癌 是高度可预防的,但它仍然是一个主要的公共卫生问题,特别是在叙利亚等资源匮乏的地区。
设计与对象
对居住在大马士革和大马士革农村地区 16 岁或以上的叙利亚妇女进行了一项横断面研究,使用了一份改编自 CCKP-64 工具的匿名自填问卷,并使用滚雪球抽样技术,于 2025 年 12 月 1 日至 2026 年 3 月 1 日通过社交媒体平台在线分发。
核心方法
数据收集自 405 名叙利亚妇女,并使用 IBM SPSS Statistics v25 进行数据分析。
主要结果
尽管大多数参与者认识到 宫颈癌 可能与感染有关 (86.2%),并且对子宫颈抹片检查有一定了解 (59.8%),但实际筛查率很低,只有 6.9% 的人进行过筛查。对风险因素的了解程度中等(平均分:49.16 ± 8.14(满分 85 分)),但对于尚未确定的风险因素仍然存在误解。多变量分析显示,父母医疗保健就业和 16-19 岁以上年龄组独立预测对风险因素的更高了解(p < 0.05)。

叙利亚妇女对 宫颈癌 的高度认识并没有转化为预防行动,这证实仅靠知识是不够的。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要将 HPV 疫苗接种 纳入国家免疫规划,同时开展有针对性的教育工作,以改善预防措施。
局限与证据强度
观察到知识与行动之间的差距,表明仅靠意识并不能确保预防行为,并且由于横截面设计和自我报告数据中的潜在偏差,证据是温和的。

Awareness of cervical cancer, human papillomavirus vaccination, and cervical cancer screening among syrian women: a cross-sectional study

Authors: Ahmad Nabil Alhouri, Rami Akwan, Farah Asif Sahloul, Dania Taisir Keshkeh, Fatima Alhouri, Abir Koudsi
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer, despite being highly preventable, remains a major public health concern, particularly in low-resource settings such as Syria. Due to limited national data, we aimed to evaluate the awareness of Syrian women regarding cervical cancer and its prevention strategies, as well as their practice of these methods. A cross-sectional study was conducted among Syrian women aged 16 years or older residing in Damascus and Rif Dimashq, using an anonymous, self-administered questionnaire adapted from the CCKP-64 instrument and distributed online from 1 December 2025 to 1 March 2026 via social media platforms using a snowball sampling technique. Data analysis was performed with IBM SPSS Statistics v25. Data were collected from 405 Syrian women. A knowledge-to-action gap was observed. Despite most participants recognizing that cervical cancer can be linked to an infection (86.2%) and moderate familiarity with Pap smear (59.8%), actual screening rates were low, with only 6.9% ever screened. HPV vaccine awareness was limited (35.1%), with an even smaller proportion vaccinated (3%), and only 11.6% correctly identified the optimal age range for vaccination as 9 to 13 years. Notably, only a small proportion of participants (9.1%) had direct contact with affected individuals, and 31.9% thought the disease could affect them in the future. Knowledge of risk factors was moderate (mean score: 49.16 ± 8.14 out of 85), yet misconceptions persisted regarding non-established risk factors. The multivariate analysis showed that parental healthcare employment and age groups older than 16–19 years independently predicted higher knowledge of risk factors ( p < 0.05). High cervical cancer awareness among Syrian women did not translate into preventive action, confirming that knowledge alone is insufficient; therefore, integrating HPV vaccination into the national immunisation program alongside targeted educational efforts is crucially needed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer, despite being highly preventable, remains a major public health concern, particularly in low-resource settings such as Syria.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among Syrian women aged 16 years or older residing in Damascus and Rif Dimashq, using an anonymous, self-administered questionnaire adapted from the CCKP-64 instrument and distributed online from 1 December 2025 to 1 March 2026 via social media platforms using a snowball sampling technique.
Core methods
Data were collected from 405 Syrian women, and data analysis was performed with IBM SPSS Statistics v25.
Main results
Despite most participants recognizing that cervical cancer can be linked to an infection (86.2%) and moderate familiarity with Pap smear (59.8%), actual screening rates were low, with only 6.9% ever screened. Knowledge of risk factors was moderate (mean score: 49.16 ± 8.14 out of 85), yet misconceptions persisted regarding non-established risk factors. The multivariate analysis showed that parental healthcare employment and age groups older than 16–19 years independently predicted higher knowledge of risk factors (p < 0.05).
Interpretation and novelty
High cervical cancer awareness among Syrian women did not translate into preventive action, confirming that knowledge alone is insufficient.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for integrating HPV vaccination into the national immunisation program alongside targeted educational efforts to improve preventive actions.
Limitations and evidence strength
A knowledge-to-action gap was observed, indicating that awareness alone does not ensure preventive behavior, and the evidence is moderate due to the cross-sectional design and potential biases in self-reported data.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

哈萨克斯坦阿斯塔纳单一初级保健中心的女性对 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 的知识、信任和多维认知:一项横断面研究

Knowledge, trust, and multidimensional perceptions of human papillomavirus vaccination among women attending a single primary care centre in Astana, Kazakhstan: a cross-sectional study
作者： Ayagyoz Umbetzhanova, Adina Kusniyeva, Gulmira Derbissalina, Aigul Abduldayeva, Nikolay Safonov, Vitaliy Koikov
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探索 2024 年重新引入 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 后,哈萨克斯坦阿斯塔纳女性对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 的知识、信任和多维认知,因为公众信心和沟通对于 疫苗 的接受至关重要。
设计与对象
2026 年 1 月 17 日至 2 月 28 日,对哈萨克斯坦阿斯塔纳单一初级保健中心的 221 名成年女性进行了一项横断面电子调查。
核心方法
通过调查评估了 HPV 知识、特定来源的信任度和 16 个认知项目。探索性心理测量分析采用多相关性、平行分析和主轴因子分析与obminin旋转来得出综合测量。使用斯皮尔曼相关性和稳健的多变量线性模型来检查安全性问题和感知有效性。
主要结果
12 项中的平均 HPV 知识为 4.95 ± 3.84。得出了五项安全关注指标(平均值 2.34 ± 0.97;α = 0.868;ω = 0.901)和三项感知有效性指标(平均值 3.20 ± 1.24;α = 0.922;ω = 0.948)。知识与安全担忧呈负相关(ρ = -0.284,p < 0.001),与感知有效性呈正相关(ρ = 0.393,p < 0.001)。安全问题与 COVID-19 争议 跨物种溢出、早期计划的不信任以及对自然免疫的偏好有关。更高的知识和专业/科学信任与更高的感知有效性相关。
解释与创新
作者解释说,HPV 疫苗 的认知是多维的,知识和专业科学信任主要与感知有效性相关,而安全问题与 疫苗 叙述更密切相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要采取将事实教育与透明的安全对话和临床医生沟通相结合的沟通策略,以提高 HPV 疫苗接种 的接受度并解决哈萨克斯坦阿斯塔纳妇女的担忧。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计和方便抽样的限制,这可能会影响普遍性。证据是中等的,因为结果是基于自我报告的数据和探索性分析。

Knowledge, trust, and multidimensional perceptions of human papillomavirus vaccination among women attending a single primary care centre in Astana, Kazakhstan: a cross-sectional study

Authors: Ayagyoz Umbetzhanova, Adina Kusniyeva, Gulmira Derbissalina, Aigul Abduldayeva, Nikolay Safonov, Vitaliy Koikov
Original abstract (English)
Background Kazakhstan reintroduced human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination in 2024, making public confidence and communication important. This study described HPV knowledge and trust in information sources and exploratorily examined vaccine-perception structure and correlates among women attending primary care in Astana. Methods A cross-sectional electronic survey was conducted from 17 January to 28 February 2026 among 221 adult women recruited through convenience sampling. HPV knowledge, source-specific trust, and 16 perception items were assessed. Exploratory psychometric analysis used polychoric correlations, parallel analysis, and principal-axis factor analysis with oblimin rotation to derive preliminary composite measures. Spearman correlations and robust multivariable linear models examined safety concern and perceived effectiveness. A secondary logistic model examined refusal or intention to refuse HPV vaccination for a daughter. Results Mean HPV knowledge was 4.95 ± 3.84 of 12. A five-item safety concern measure (mean 2.34 ± 0.97; α = 0.868; ω = 0.901) and a three-item perceived effectiveness measure (mean 3.20 ± 1.24; α = 0.922; ω = 0.948) were derived. Knowledge correlated negatively with safety concern ( ρ = −0.284, p < 0.001) and positively with perceived effectiveness ( ρ = 0.393, p < 0.001). Safety concern was associated with COVID-19 controversy spillover (B = 0.190, 95% CI 0.043−0.337), earlier-programme distrust (B = 0.197, 95% CI 0.047−0.347), and preference for natural immunity (B = 0.137, 95% CI 0.040−0.234). Higher knowledge (B = 0.061, 95% CI 0.010−0.111) and professional/scientific trust (B = 0.297, 95% CI 0.114−0.480) were associated with greater perceived effectiveness. Forty-nine participants (22.2%) reported refusal or intention to refuse vaccination for a daughter; knowing someone believed to have become seriously ill after vaccination was associated with this response (aOR 7.42, 95% CI 2.60−21.16). Conclusion HPV vaccine perceptions were multidimensional. Knowledge and professional-scientific trust were linked mainly to perceived effectiveness, whereas safety concerns were more strongly related to vaccine narratives. Communication strategies should combine factual education with transparent safety dialogue and clinician communication.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to explore knowledge, trust, and multidimensional perceptions of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination among women in Astana, Kazakhstan, following its reintroduction in 2024, as public confidence and communication are critical for vaccine acceptance.
Design and population
A cross-sectional electronic survey was conducted from 17 January to 28 February 2026 among 221 adult women attending a single primary care centre in Astana, Kazakhstan.
Core methods
HPV knowledge, source-specific trust, and 16 perception items were assessed using a survey. Exploratory psychometric analysis employed polychoric correlations, parallel analysis, and principal-axis factor analysis with oblimin rotation to derive composite measures. Spearman correlations and robust multivariable linear models were used to examine safety concern and perceived effectiveness.
Main results
Mean HPV knowledge was 4.95 ± 3.84 of 12. A five-item safety concern measure (mean 2.34 ± 0.97; α = 0.868; ω = 0.901) and a three-item perceived effectiveness measure (mean 3.20 ± 1.24; α = 0.922; ω = 0.948) were derived. Knowledge correlated negatively with safety concern (ρ = −0.284, p < 0.001) and positively with perceived effectiveness (ρ = 0.393, p < 0.001). Safety concern was associated with COVID-19 controversy spillover, earlier-programme distrust, and preference for natural immunity. Higher knowledge and professional/scientific trust were linked to greater perceived effectiveness.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that HPV vaccine perceptions were multidimensional, with knowledge and professional-scientific trust mainly linked to perceived effectiveness, whereas safety concerns were more strongly related to vaccine narratives.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for communication strategies that combine factual education with transparent safety dialogue and clinician communication to improve HPV vaccination acceptance and address concerns among women in Astana, Kazakhstan.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and convenience sampling, which may affect generalisability. The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on self-reported data and exploratory analyses.

唾液腺肿瘤组织中DAPK基因甲基化状态的测定及其与HPV 16和18阳性的关系

Determination of methylation status of DAPK gene in salivary gland tumour tissues and its association with HPV 16 & 18 positivity
作者： Anzila Meer, Sarina Sulong, Anani Aila Mat Zin, Rohimah Mohamud, Norhafiza Mat Lazim
摘要中文翻译摘要 唾液腺肿瘤(SGT)是一种异质性肿瘤,由于形态多样性而具有诊断挑战。本研究探讨了SGT中DAPK基因的甲基化状态及其与HPV16/18和p16蛋白表达的关系。使用免疫组织化学和甲基化特异性 PCR 分析了总共 45 个福尔马林固定、石蜡包埋的 SGT 样本。其中良性病例占66.67%,恶性病例占33.34%。DAPK 甲基化与肿瘤类型相关,但与 HPV 标记无关。总体而言,HPV16 L1、HPV16/18 E6 和 p16 表达分别在 68%、88% 和 77% 的病例中观察到,在良性肿瘤中出现频率更高。通过层次聚类观察到的模式表明良性和恶性 SGT 之间的分子谱存在潜在差异;然而,这些发现仍然是探索性的,因为这种分布偏离了已建立的 HPV 生物学,其中 E6 表达通常与恶性肿瘤相关,表明潜在的非特异性免疫反应性或方法学局限性。总体而言,DAPK 甲基化在样本中表现出异质模式,表明表观遗传改变可能独立于 SGT 中 HPV 驱动的机制而发生。虽然观察到了不同的分子模式,但它们的生物学意义仍然不确定。总之,这项研究表明 DAPK 甲基化与 SGT 中的肿瘤类型之间存在关联,但不支持 HPV 在驱动这些表观遗传变化中的明确作用。研究结果还强调了在没有 HPV 分子确认的情况下依赖 IHC 的局限性。需要进一步研究结合基于 PCR 的 HPV 分型、功能验证和更大的 队列研究 来阐明这些关系。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查唾液腺肿瘤中 DAPK 基因的甲基化状态及其与 HPV16/18 阳性的潜在关联,解决由于形态多样性而诊断这些肿瘤的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究使用免疫组织化学和甲基化特异性 PCR 分析了 45 个福尔马林固定、石蜡包埋的唾液腺肿瘤样本,以探讨 DAPK 甲基化与 HPV 标记物之间的关系。
核心方法
使用甲基化特异性 PCR 测定 DAPK 基因的甲基化状态,同时通过免疫组织化学评估 HPV16/18 阳性和 p16 蛋白表达。
主要结果
DAPK 甲基化与肿瘤类型相关,但与 HPV 标记无关。HPV16 L1、HPV16/18 E6 和 p16 表达分别在 68%、88% 和 77% 的病例中观察到,在良性肿瘤中出现频率更高。
解释与创新
作者解释说,DAPK 甲基化与唾液腺肿瘤中的肿瘤类型有关,但他们没有发现与 HPV 标记物的明确关联,这表明表观遗传变化可能独立于 HPV 驱动的机制而发生。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于了解唾液腺肿瘤的分子谱,并强调需要进一步研究以阐明 HPV 在这些疾病中的作用。
局限与证据强度
由于潜在的非特异性免疫反应性或方法学限制,该研究的结果仍然是探索性的,并且在没有分子 HPV 确认的情况下依赖免疫组织化学可能会影响结果的可靠性。

Determination of methylation status of DAPK gene in salivary gland tumour tissues and its association with HPV 16 & 18 positivity

Authors: Anzila Meer, Sarina Sulong, Anani Aila Mat Zin, Rohimah Mohamud, Norhafiza Mat Lazim
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Salivary gland tumours (SGTs) are heterogeneous neoplasms with diagnostic challenges due to morphological diversity. This study explored methylation status of DAPK gene in SGTs and its association with HPV16/18 and p16 protein expression. A total of 45 formalin-fixed, paraffin-embedded SGT samples were analysed using immunohistochemistry and methylation-specific PCR. Among the cases, 66.67% were benign and 33.34% malignant. DAPK methylation was associated with tumour type but not with HPV markers. Overall, HPV16 L1, HPV16/18 E6, and p16 expression were observed in 68%, 88%, and 77% of cases, respectively, with a higher frequency noted in benign tumours. Patterns observed through hierarchical clustering suggested potential differences in molecular profiles between benign and malignant SGTs; however, these findings remain exploratory, as this distribution deviates from established HPV biology, where E6 expression is typically associated with malignancy, suggesting potential non-specific immunoreactivity or methodological limitations. Overall, DAPK methylation exhibited a heterogeneous pattern across samples, indicating that epigenetic alterations may occur independently of HPV-driven mechanisms in SGTs. While distinct molecular patterns were observed, their biological significance remains uncertain. In conclusion, this study shows an association between DAPK methylation and tumour type in SGTs but does not support a definitive role of HPV in driving these epigenetic changes. The findings also highlight limitations associated with reliance on IHC without molecular HPV confirmation. Further studies incorporating PCR-based HPV typing, functional validation, and larger cohorts are required to clarify these relationships.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the methylation status of the DAPK gene in salivary gland tumours and its potential association with HPV16/18 positivity, addressing the challenge of diagnosing these tumours due to their morphological diversity.
Design and population
The study analysed 45 formalin-fixed, paraffin-embedded salivary gland tumour samples using immunohistochemistry and methylation-specific PCR to explore the relationship between DAPK methylation and HPV markers.
Core methods
The methylation status of the DAPK gene was determined using methylation-specific PCR, while HPV16/18 positivity and p16 protein expression were assessed via immunohistochemistry.
Main results
DAPK methylation was associated with tumour type but not with HPV markers. HPV16 L1, HPV16/18 E6, and p16 expression were observed in 68%, 88%, and 77% of cases, respectively, with a higher frequency noted in benign tumours.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that DAPK methylation is linked to tumour type in salivary gland tumours, but they did not find a definitive association with HPV markers, suggesting that epigenetic changes may occur independently of HPV-driven mechanisms.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the understanding of molecular profiles in salivary gland tumours and highlight the need for further studies to clarify the role of HPV in these conditions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's findings remain exploratory due to potential non-specific immunoreactivity or methodological limitations, and the reliance on immunohistochemistry without molecular HPV confirmation may affect the reliability of the results.

福建省莆田市男性门诊患者患病率及人乳头瘤病毒感染基因型分布特征的回顾性研究

Prevalence and genotype distribution characteristics of human papillomavirus infection among male outpatients in Putian, Fujian Province: a retrospective study
作者： Yan Chen, Guo-Long Xiao, Ai-Min Chen, Ru-Jing Zhang, Lin-Lin Zhang
摘要中文翻译此前针对莆田地区男性人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 患病率的研究尚未见报道。本研究旨在调查福建省莆田地区男性HPV流行病学患病率及基因型分布,为优化男性HPV筛查和区域预防策略提供经验依据。回顾性纳入2023年5月至2026年2月莆田市第一医院门诊男性患者731例。根据主要临床诊断将患者分为6个亚组,包括疣、尿路感染(UTI)、皮炎、生殖器疾病、健康检查等。采用聚合酶链式反应(PCR)扩增结合流通杂交技术对21种HPV基因型进行检测。HPV 患病率 总体率为 35.2%,其中单基因型感染率为 22.7%,多基因型感染率为 12.5%,患病率 在年龄 > 60 岁的患者中观察到最高。最常见的五种基因型是 HPV6、HPV52、HPV16、HPV51 和 HPV42,与之前报道的本地女性的主要基因型有很大重叠,包括低危 HPV (LR-HPV) 型 HPV6、HPV42 和 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 型 HPV52、HPV51 和 HPV16。此外,HPV 患病率 在不同的临床诊断中存在显着差异 (p = 0.019);“疣”组的感染率最高(48.7%,主要是HPV6/11),其次是皮炎组(46.5%,表现出高基因型多样性,包括HPV52、16、42、51和59)。多变量回归分析确定皮炎是 HR-HPV 感染的独立危险因素。值得注意的是,即使使用 9 价 (9 v) HPV 疫苗,总体 HPV 阳性和 HR-HPV 队列研究 之间的覆盖率差距仍然分别为 33.5% 和 33.9%。莆田地区的男性门诊患者表现出高HPV 患病率,且具有明显的年龄和疾病特异性基因型分布。男性门诊患者和当地女性之间主要 HPV 基因型的大量重叠凸显了将男性纳入 HPV 预防策略的重要性。这一发现可能支持未来对以合作伙伴为导向的预防方法的评估。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究针对福建省莆田市男性HPV 患病率前期研究的不足,旨在调查该地区男性HPV 患病率的流行病学情况及基因型分布。
设计与对象
方法回顾性研究2023年5月至2026年2月莆田市第一医院门诊男性患者731例。根据患者的主要临床诊断,将患者分为六个亚组。
核心方法
采用聚合酶链式反应(PCR)扩增结合流通杂交技术对21种HPV基因型进行检测。使用多变量回归分析来确定 HR-HPV 感染的危险因素。
主要结果
HPV 患病率 总体感染率为 35.2%,其中单基因型感染率为 22.7%,多基因型感染率为 12.5%。最常见的五种基因型是 HPV6、HPV52、HPV16、HPV51 和 HPV42,与之前报道的当地女性的主要基因型有很大重叠。
解释与创新
该研究强调了莆田地区男性门诊患者中不同年龄和疾病特异性的基因型分布,为男性HPV感染的流行病学模式提供了经验证据。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了将男性纳入 HPV 预防策略的重要性,因为男性和女性之间主要基因型的大量重叠表明共同的风险因素和以伴侣为导向的预防方法的潜力。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的选择偏倚而受到限制,但由于使用PCR和流通杂交技术进行基因型检测以及使用多变量回归分析进行危险因素识别,因此证据充足。

Prevalence and genotype distribution characteristics of human papillomavirus infection among male outpatients in Putian, Fujian Province: a retrospective study

Authors: Yan Chen, Guo-Long Xiao, Ai-Min Chen, Ru-Jing Zhang, Lin-Lin Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Previous research focusing on male human papillomavirus (HPV) prevalence within Putian has not yet been reported. This study aims to investigate the epidemiological prevalence and genotype distribution of HPV among males in the Putian region of Fujian Province, providing empirical evidence to optimize male HPV screening and regional prevention strategies. A total of 731 male outpatients from the First Hospital of Putian city were retrospectively enrolled between May 2023 and February 2026. The enrolled patients were stratified into six subgroups according to their primary clinical diagnoses, including warts, urinary tract infections (UTIs), dermatitis, genital diseases, health check-ups, and others. The detection of 21 HPV genotypes was performed using polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplification combined with flow-through hybridization technology. The overall HPV prevalence was 35.2%, including 22.7% single-genotype infections and 12.5% multiple-genotype infections, with the highest observed prevalence in patients aged > 60 years. The five most frequent genotypes were HPV6, HPV52, HPV16, HPV51, and HPV42, showing substantial overlap with the predominant genotypes previously reported in local females, including low-risk HPV (LR-HPV) types HPV6, HPV42 and high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) types HPV52, HPV51, and HPV16. Furthermore, HPV prevalence varied significantly across distinct clinical diagnoses ( p = 0.019); the “warts” group exhibited the highest rate (48.7%, predominantly HPV6/11), followed by the dermatitis group (46.5%, displaying high genotype diversity including HPV52, 16, 42, 51, and 59). Multivariable regression analysis identified dermatitis as an independent risk factor for HR-HPV infection. Notably, even with the 9-valent (9 v) HPV vaccine, substantial coverage gaps of 33.5% and 33.9% persisted among the overall HPV-positive and HR-HPV cohorts, respectively. Male outpatients in the Putian region exhibit a high HPV prevalence with distinct age- and disease-specific genotype distributions. The substantial overlap in predominant HPV genotypes between male outpatients and local females highlights the importance of including men in HPV prevention strategies. This finding may support future evaluation of partner-oriented prevention approaches.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study was needed to address the lack of prior research on male HPV prevalence in Putian, Fujian Province, and it aimed to investigate the epidemiological prevalence and genotype distribution of HPV among males in the region.
Design and population
A retrospective study was conducted from May 2023 to February 2026, enrolling 731 male outpatients from the First Hospital of Putian city. The patients were stratified into six subgroups based on their primary clinical diagnoses.
Core methods
The detection of 21 HPV genotypes was performed using polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplification combined with flow-through hybridization technology. Multivariable regression analysis was used to identify risk factors for HR-HPV infection.
Main results
The overall HPV prevalence was 35.2%, with 22.7% single-genotype infections and 12.5% multiple-genotype infections. The five most frequent genotypes were HPV6, HPV52, HPV16, HPV51, and HPV42, showing substantial overlap with predominant genotypes previously reported in local females.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the distinct age- and disease-specific genotype distributions among male outpatients in the Putian region, providing empirical evidence on the epidemiological patterns of HPV infection in males.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the importance of including men in HPV prevention strategies, as the substantial overlap in predominant genotypes between males and females suggests shared risk factors and potential for partner-oriented prevention approaches.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for selection bias, but the evidence is strong due to the use of PCR and flow-through hybridization technology for genotype detection and multivariable regression analysis for risk factor identification.

2023-2025年中国徐州15,224例妇科门诊患者患病率及人乳头瘤病毒基因型分布

Prevalence and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus among 15,224 gynecological outpatients in Xuzhou, China, during 2023–2025
作者： Shu-Chen Chai, Lu-Lu Hu, Wenting Fan, Ying Li, Zhi-Jian Liu, Zi-Wei Song, Shi-Bao Li, Ying-Xing Zhu, Fan Wenting, ZHU Yingxing
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查妇科门诊患者中的 患病率 和 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 基因型分布,为特定地区的 宫颈癌 预防策略提供参考。
设计与对象
该研究分析了2023年至2025年徐州医科大学附属医院15224名妇科门诊患者的HPV 患病率和基因型分布,并按年龄组和年份进行分层。
核心方法
使用聚合酶链式反应 (PCR) 确定 HPV 亚型,并分析不同年龄组和年份的 患病率 和基因型分布。
主要结果
在15,224名门诊患者中,4,884名(32.08%)HPV检测呈阳性,其中单一高危型HPV(HR-HPV)感染是主要模式(16.48%)。HR-HPV 基因型前五位分别为 HPV52(5.58%)、16(4.93%)、58(4.49%)、53(3.54%)和 56(2.51%)。排名前三位的低危HPV(LR-HPV)基因型分别为HPV42(3.30%)、81(2.16%)和43(1.76%)。
解释与创新
观察到的HPV基因型的年龄分布表明,年轻女性和中老年女性可能是未来预防策略的重要目标群体。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要针对非 疫苗 覆盖的 HPV 基因型制定有针对性的预防策略和持续 监测,特别是在年轻和中年女性中。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据基于单个机构的门诊数据,这可能会限制普遍性。总体 HPV 患病率 在不同年龄组之间存在显着差异 (p < 0.001),表明与年龄有很强的相关性。

Prevalence and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus among 15,224 gynecological outpatients in Xuzhou, China, during 2023–2025

Authors: Shu-Chen Chai, Lu-Lu Hu, Wenting Fan, Ying Li, Zhi-Jian Liu, Zi-Wei Song, Shi-Bao Li, Ying-Xing Zhu, Fan Wenting, ZHU Yingxing
Original abstract (English)
Objective This study investigated HPV prevalence and genotype distribution among gynecological outpatients at the Affiliated Hospital of Xuzhou Medical University, aiming to inform region-specific cervical cancer prevention strategies. Methods We determined the HPV subtypes by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) and analyzed the HPV prevalence, genotype distribution in 15,224 gynecological outpatients by age group and year. Results Among the 15,224 outpatients, 4,884 (32.08%) tested positive for HPV, with single high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) infection being the predominant pattern (16.48%). The top five HR-HPV genotypes were HPV52 (5.58%), 16 (4.93%), 58 (4.49%), 53 (3.54%), and 56 (2.51%). The top three low-risk HPV (LR-HPV) genotypes were HPV42 (3.30%), 81 (2.16%), and 43 (1.76%). From 2023 to 2025, the overall HPV prevalence among this study population exhibited a significant increasing trend, rising from 27.57 to 36.63% ( p(linear by linear) < 0.001 ). Overall HPV prevalence differed significantly across age groups ( p < 0.001 ), exhibiting a bimodal distribution, with the highest rate in the ≤24 years group (42.30%), followed by the 55–64 years group (37.29%) and the ≥65 years group (36.43%), and the lowest in the 25–34 years group (29.05%). Across age groups, the top five HR-HPV genotypes varied slightly but consistently included HPV52, 16, 58, 53, and 56. For LR-HPV infections, the top three genotypes were HPV6, 42, and 11 in the ≤24 years group, whereas HPV42, 81, and 43 ranked as the top three in all other age groups. Conclusion The prevalence of HPV infection among women attending gynecological outpatient clinics of the Affiliated Hospital of Xuzhou Medical University is relatively high and has demonstrated an increasing trend over the study period. Adolescents and middle-aged and older women exhibit higher HPV prevalence. The predominant genotypes are HR-HPV52, 16, 58, 53, and 56, as well as LR-HPV42, 81, and 43. In addition to strengthening universal health education, the observed age-specific distribution suggests that young women and middle-aged and older women may be important target groups for future prevention strategies. Furthermore, the high prevalence of non-vaccine-covered genotypes (HPV53, 56, 42, 81, and 43) warrants continued surveillance and further investigation of their clinical significance, although these implications should be considered preliminary.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the prevalence and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus (HPV) among gynecological outpatients to inform region-specific cervical cancer prevention strategies.
Design and population
The study analyzed HPV prevalence and genotype distribution in 15,224 gynecological outpatients at the Affiliated Hospital of Xuzhou Medical University from 2023 to 2025, stratified by age group and year.
Core methods
HPV subtypes were determined using polymerase chain reaction (PCR), and the prevalence and genotype distribution were analyzed across age groups and years.
Main results
Among the 15,224 outpatients, 4,884 (32.08%) tested positive for HPV, with single high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) infection being the predominant pattern (16.48%). The top five HR-HPV genotypes were HPV52 (5.58%), 16 (4.93%), 58 (4.49%), 53 (3.54%), and 56 (2.51%). The top three low-risk HPV (LR-HPV) genotypes were HPV42 (3.30%), 81 (2.16%), and 43 (1.76%).
Interpretation and novelty
The observed age-specific distribution of HPV genotypes suggests that young women and middle-aged and older women may be important target groups for future prevention strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for targeted prevention strategies and continued surveillance of non-vaccine-covered HPV genotypes, particularly in young and middle-aged women.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is based on a single institution's outpatient data, which may limit generalizability. The overall HPV prevalence differed significantly across age groups (p < 0.001), indicating a strong association with age.

中国山东省大学生对尖锐湿疣和HPV 疫苗接种的认知、态度和行为

Perceived knowledge, attitudes, and practices toward genital warts and HPV vaccination among college students in Shandong Province, China
作者： Min Zhu, Gui-Lin Xu, Ning-Feng Li, Xin-Li Tian
摘要中文翻译目的 调查山东省大学生对尖锐湿疣和HPV 疫苗接种的认知、态度和行为。方法对2026年3月13日至4月2日在中国山东省四所大学的中国大学生进行横断面研究。数据采用中文自填问卷收集,该问卷是为本研究新开发的,涵盖人口特征和知识-态度-实践(KAP)框架的三个维度:感知知识(14个项目,得分范围0-28)、态度(9个项目,得分范围9-45)和实践(10个项目,得分范围10-50)。布鲁姆截止标准适用于每个量表的可达到范围,计算公式为(观察分数 - 最低可能分数)/(最大可能分数 - 最低可能分数)× 100%,分数分为差(< 60%)、中等(60%−79%)或良好(≥80%)。结构方程模型(SEM)用于检查感知知识、态度和实践之间的关联。结果共纳入959名参与者,其中男性占58.39%。感知知识、态度和实践的平均得分分别为 15.34 ± 7.64、35.92 ± 7.42 和 36.84 ± 11.71,对应于每个量表可达到范围的 54.79%、74.78% 和 67.10%,因此属于感知知识较差、态度和实践中等的情况。在SEM中,感知知识与态度(β = 0.119,P = 0.012)和实践(β = 0.461,P = 0.014)直接相关,态度与实践直接相关(β = 0.118,P = 0.007)。还观察到通过态度感知的知识和实践之间存在间接关联(β = 0.014,P = 0.003)。感知知识、态度和实践得分均呈正相关(均 P < 0.001)。结论 山东省大学生对尖锐湿疣和HPV 疫苗接种的认知水平较差,态度和行为较差。在指定的 SEM 中,感知知识显示出与实践结构的最强关联,而态度则通过态度起到了适度的间接关联的作用。因为设计是横截面的;这些发现代表的是关联性而不是因果效应。校园性健康教育应优先考虑准确的 HPV 相关知识、男性 疫苗接种 意识以及可访问的 STI 检查和 HPV 疫苗接种 转诊途径。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查中国山东省大学生对尖锐湿疣和HPV 疫苗接种的认知、态度和行为。
设计与对象
2026年3月13日至4月2日,对中国山东省四所大学的中国大学生进行了一项横断面研究。共纳入 959 名参与者,其中男性占 58.39%。
核心方法
数据采用中文自填问卷收集,该问卷是为本研究新开发的,涵盖人口特征和知识-态度-实践(KAP)框架的三个维度:感知知识(14个项目,得分范围0-28)、态度(9个项目,得分范围9-45)和实践(10个项目,得分范围10-50)。
主要结果
感知知识、态度和实践的平均得分分别为 15.34 ± 7.64、35.92 ± 7.42 和 36.84 ± 11.71,对应于每个量表可达到范围的 54.79%、74.78% 和 67.10%,因此属于感知知识较差、态度和实践中等的情况。
解释与创新
感知知识与态度(β = 0.119,P = 0.012)和实践(β = 0.461,P = 0.014)直接相关,态度与实践直接相关(β = 0.118,P = 0.007)。还观察到通过态度感知的知识和实践之间存在间接关联(β = 0.014,P = 0.003)。
科研与公卫意义
校园性健康教育应优先考虑准确的 HPV 相关知识、男性 疫苗接种 意识以及可访问的 STI 检查和 HPV 疫苗接种 转诊途径。
局限与证据强度
因为设计是横截面的;这些发现代表的是关联性而不是因果效应。

Perceived knowledge, attitudes, and practices toward genital warts and HPV vaccination among college students in Shandong Province, China

Authors: Min Zhu, Gui-Lin Xu, Ning-Feng Li, Xin-Li Tian
Original abstract (English)
Objective This study aimed to investigate the perceived knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding genital warts and HPV vaccination among college students in Shandong Province, China. Methods A cross-sectional study was conducted on Chinese college students from March 13 to April 2, 2026, in four universities in Shandong Province, China. Data were collected using a self-administered questionnaire in Chinese that was newly developed for this study and covered demographic characteristics and the three dimensions of the knowledge–attitude–practice (KAP) framework: perceived knowledge (14 items, score range 0–28), attitude (9 items, score range 9–45), and practice (10 items, score range 10–50). Bloom's cut-off criteria were applied to the attainable range of each scale, calculated as (observed score – minimum possible score) / (maximum possible score – minimum possible score) × 100%, and scores were classified as poor (< 60%), moderate (60%−79%), or good (≥80%). Structural equation modeling (SEM) was used to examine the associations among perceived knowledge, attitude, and practice. Results A total of 959 participants were included, with 58.39% of participants being male. The mean perceived knowledge, attitude, and practice scores were 15.34 ± 7.64, 35.92 ± 7.42, and 36.84 ± 11.71, corresponding to 54.79%, 74.78%, and 67.10% of the attainable range of each scale, and therefore to poor perceived knowledge and moderate attitude and practice. In the SEM, perceived knowledge was directly associated with attitude (β = 0.119, P = 0.012) and with practice (β = 0.461, P = 0.014), and attitude was directly associated with practice (β = 0.118, P = 0.007). An indirect association between perceived knowledge and practice through attitude was also observed (β = 0.014, P = 0.003). Perceived knowledge, attitude, and practice scores were positively correlated with each other (all P < 0.001). Conclusion College students in Shandong Province, China had poor perceived knowledge and moderate attitudes and practices regarding genital warts and HPV vaccination. Perceived knowledge showed the strongest association with the practice construct in the specified SEM, with attitude acting as a modest indirect association through attitude. Because the design was cross-sectional; these findings represent associations rather than causal effects. Campus-based sexual health education should prioritize accurate HPV-related knowledge, male vaccination awareness, and accessible referral pathways for STI examination and HPV vaccination.

Main results
The mean perceived knowledge, attitude, and practice scores were 15.34 ± 7.64, 35.92 ± 7.42, and 36.84 ± 11.71, corresponding to 54.79%, 74.78%, and 67.10% of the attainable range of each scale, and therefore to poor perceived knowledge and moderate attitude and practice.

Because the design was cross-sectional; these findings represent associations rather than causal effects.

葡萄牙 人乳头瘤病毒 自我抽样筛查 宫颈癌:社会弱势移民妇女的混合方法可接受性研究

Human papillomavirus self-sampling for cervical cancer screening in Portugal: a mixed-methods acceptability study among socially vulnerable migrant women
作者： Walaa Kinaan, Ana Filipa Gama, Patrícia Marques, Bruno Heleno, Sónia Dias
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 宫颈癌 是一个公共卫生问题。尽管可以通过定期筛查来预防,但由于传统筛查计划存在障碍,生活在社会弱势群体的妇女和移民对筛查计划的参与仍然不够理想。因此,这些女性的 发病率 和 死亡率 率不成比例地较高,凸显了 宫颈癌 结果的不平等。人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 自我采样已成为一种有前途的策略,可以减轻这些障碍并增加获得预防性生殖健康服务的机会。然而,其在社区环境中可接受性的证据有限。本研究借鉴适应文化的 HPV 自我采样干预措施,旨在探讨里斯本社区环境中社会弱势移民妇女对 HPV 自我采样的接受程度。方法 我们对 111 名 30-65 岁的社会弱势移民女性进行了一项序贯混合方法研究,她们参加了里斯本社区组织提供的 HPV 自我采样干预措施。定量部分包括评估 HPV 自我采样的预期和经验可接受性的事前调查问卷。定性部分包括与八名干预参与者进行小型焦点小组讨论,以更深入地探索经验。使用 R 中的 Wilcoxon 符号秩检验对定量数据进行分析,并根据可接受性理论框架对定性数据进行主题分析。结果 我们的研究结果表明 HPV 自我采样的接受度很高。前后比较显示,在对测试结果的信任度、感知的轻松度和舒适度、对自我采样的信心以及过程速度方面发生了统计上显着的变化(p < 0.05),同时疼痛、紧张、尴尬和对帮助的需求也减少了。定性数据体现了这些发现:女性重视隐私、简单和信任。然而,人们发现了对正确测试性能和阳性结果的担忧。明确的指示和社区设置被视为推动因素。结论 HPV 自我采样在社会弱势流动妇女中被认为是可以接受的。这些发现可能会为 宫颈癌 筛查实施的考虑提供参考,该筛查旨在扩大 宫颈癌 预防的可及性,涉及如何在受信任的社区环境中引入、促进和支持 HPV 自我采样。需要进一步的研究来评估感知的可接受性是否转化为在不同实施环境中持续的筛查采用和公平的筛查参与。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 是一个公共卫生问题。尽管可以通过定期筛查来预防,但由于传统筛查计划存在障碍,生活在社会弱势群体的妇女和移民对筛查计划的参与仍然不够理想。
设计与对象
本研究借鉴适应文化的 HPV 自我采样干预措施,旨在探讨里斯本社区环境中社会弱势移民妇女对 HPV 自我采样的接受程度。我们对 111 名 30-65 岁的社会弱势移民女性进行了一项序贯混合方法研究,她们参加了里斯本社区组织提供的 HPV 自我采样干预措施。
核心方法
定量部分包括评估 HPV 自我采样的预期和经验可接受性的事前调查问卷。使用 R 中的 Wilcoxon 符号秩检验对定量数据进行分析,并根据可接受性理论框架对定性数据进行主题分析。
主要结果
我们的研究结果表明 HPV 自我采样的接受度很高。前后比较显示,在对测试结果的信任度、感知的轻松度和舒适度、对自我采样的信心以及过程速度方面发生了统计上显着的变化(p < 0.05),同时疼痛、紧张、尴尬和对帮助的需求也减少了。

HPV 自我采样被认为在社会弱势移民妇女中是可以接受的。明确的指示和社区设置被视为推动因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能会为 宫颈癌 筛查实施的考虑提供参考,该筛查旨在扩大 宫颈癌 预防的可及性,涉及如何在受信任的社区环境中引入、促进和支持 HPV 自我采样。
局限与证据强度
需要进一步的研究来评估感知的可接受性是否转化为在不同实施环境中持续的筛查采用和公平的筛查参与。

Human papillomavirus self-sampling for cervical cancer screening in Portugal: a mixed-methods acceptability study among socially vulnerable migrant women

Authors: Walaa Kinaan, Ana Filipa Gama, Patrícia Marques, Bruno Heleno, Sónia Dias
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Cervical cancer is a public health concern. Although it is preventable through regular screening, participation in screening programmes remains suboptimal among women living in socially vulnerable situations and migrants due to barriers to conventional screening programmes. Consequently, these women experience disproportionately higher incidence and mortality rates, underscoring inequities in cervical cancer outcomes. Human Papillomavirus (HPV) self-sampling has emerged as a promising strategy to mitigate these barriers and enhance access to preventive reproductive health services. However, evidence of its acceptability in community settings is limited. Drawing on a culturally adapted HPV self-sampling intervention, this study aimed to explore the acceptability of HPV self-sampling among socially vulnerable migrant women in community settings in Lisbon. Methods We conducted a sequential mixed-methods study among 111 socially vulnerable migrant women aged 30–65 years who participated in an HPV self-sampling intervention delivered through community-based organisations in Lisbon. The quantitative component consisted of a pre–post questionnaire assessing anticipated and experienced acceptability of HPV self-sampling. The qualitative component involved mini–focus group discussions with eight intervention participants to explore experiences in greater depth. Quantitative data were analysed using the Wilcoxon signed-rank test in R, and qualitative data were analysed thematically informed by the Theoretical Framework of Acceptability. Results Our findings indicate high perceived acceptability of HPV self-sampling. Pre–post comparisons showed statistically significant changes (p < 0.05) in trust in test results, perceived ease and comfort, confidence in self-sampling, and the speed of the process, alongside reductions in pain, nervousness, embarrassment, and the need for help. Qualitative data contextualised these findings: women valued the privacy, simplicity, and trust. However, concerns regarding correct test performance and positive results were identified. Clear instructions and community settings emerged as perceived enablers. Conclusion HPV self-sampling was perceived as acceptable among socially vulnerable migrant women. These findings may inform considerations for cervical cancer screening implementation aimed at expanding access to cervical cancer prevention with respect to how HPV self-sampling is introduced, facilitated, and supported within trusted community settings. Further research is needed to assess whether perceived acceptability translates into sustained screening uptake and equitable screening participation across diverse implementation settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer is a public health concern. Although it is preventable through regular screening, participation in screening programmes remains suboptimal among women living in socially vulnerable situations and migrants due to barriers to conventional screening programmes.
Design and population
Drawing on a culturally adapted HPV self-sampling intervention, this study aimed to explore the acceptability of HPV self-sampling among socially vulnerable migrant women in community settings in Lisbon. We conducted a sequential mixed-methods study among 111 socially vulnerable migrant women aged 30–65 years who participated in an HPV self-sampling intervention delivered through community-based organisations in Lisbon.
Core methods
The quantitative component consisted of a pre–post questionnaire assessing anticipated and experienced acceptability of HPV self-sampling. Quantitative data were analysed using the Wilcoxon signed-rank test in R, and qualitative data were analysed thematically informed by the Theoretical Framework of Acceptability.
Main results
Our findings indicate high perceived acceptability of HPV self-sampling. Pre–post comparisons showed statistically significant changes (p < 0.05) in trust in test results, perceived ease and comfort, confidence in self-sampling, and the speed of the process, alongside reductions in pain, nervousness, embarrassment, and the need for help.
Interpretation and novelty
HPV self-sampling was perceived as acceptable among socially vulnerable migrant women. Clear instructions and community settings emerged as perceived enablers.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform considerations for cervical cancer screening implementation aimed at expanding access to cervical cancer prevention with respect to how HPV self-sampling is introduced, facilitated, and supported within trusted community settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
Further research is needed to assess whether perceived acceptability translates into sustained screening uptake and equitable screening participation across diverse implementation settings.

自我采集阴道样本用于人乳头瘤病毒 DNA检测的可接受性和偏好性与医生采集的巴氏涂片比较

Acceptability and preference of self-collected vaginal samples for human papillomavirus DNA testing compared with physician-collected Pap smear.
作者： Ourlad Alzeus G Tantengco, Mikayla Francesca N de Guzman, Amina C Kunting, Sheriah Laine M de Paz-Silava, Michele H Diwa, Ian Kim B Tabios
摘要中文翻译背景:自我采集阴道样本用于人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)DNA检测为医生采集的巴氏涂片提供了一种有前景的替代方案,特别是在资源匮乏的环境中。然而,关于菲律宾女性对其可接受性和偏好的数据仍然有限。本研究旨在评估女性对自我采集阴道样本进行HPV DNA检测与巴氏涂片的感知、体验、意愿和偏好。方法:在592名女性中进行了一项横断面研究。参与者接受了医生采集的巴氏涂片和自我采集的阴道样本用于HPV DNA检测。使用五点李克特量表问卷在自我采集前后测量感知。采用描述性统计分析,并使用配对和独立Wilcoxon检验比较李克特量表回答。使用Cochran's Q和McNemar检验分析接受筛查的意愿。参与者偏好的筛查方法的原因被主题分类,并以堆叠条形图显示。结果:自我采集通常被认为无痛、不不适、不尴尬且容易和方便。参与者进行自我采集后,可接受性显著提高(疼痛和不适的P < 0.001)。与巴氏涂片相比,自我采集被认为更不尴尬(P < 0.001),但被认为更不便于操作(P = 0.01)和更不方便(P = 0.004)。大多数参与者由于对医疗专业知识的信任以及对程序准确性、安全性和清洁度的信心,更倾向于医生进行的筛查(84.8%)。结论:自我采集阴道样本具有高度可接受性,但医生采集的筛查仍然是首选方法。通过改善自我采集的教育、演示和支持,可能提高HPV DNA检测的参与率,并扩大菲律宾宫颈癌筛查的可及性。科学与公共卫生意义:这些发现可以为提高HPV DNA检测参与率和扩大菲律宾宫颈癌筛查的可及性提供策略依据,强调了对自我采集方法进行教育和支持的必要性。局限性与证据强度:本研究的局限性在于其横断面设计和自我报告数据,这可能引入偏倚。证据强度中等,因为它提供了关于可接受性和偏好的见解,但并未建立因果关系。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
自我采集阴道样本用于人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)DNA检测为医生采集的巴氏涂片提供了一种有前景的替代方案,特别是在资源匮乏的环境中。然而,关于菲律宾女性对其可接受性和偏好的数据仍然有限。
设计与对象
在592名女性中进行了一项横断面研究。参与者接受了医生采集的巴氏涂片和自我采集的阴道样本用于HPV DNA检测。
核心方法
感知通过五点李克特量表问卷在自我采样前和后进行测量。使用描述性统计,并采用配对和独立样本威尔科克森检验来比较李克特量表的回答。接受筛查的意愿通过科克兰Q检验和麦克纳马检验进行分析。
主要结果
自我采集通常被认为无痛、不不适、不尴尬且容易和方便。参与者进行自我采集后,可接受性显著提高(疼痛和不适的P < 0.001)。与巴氏涂片相比,自我采集被认为更不尴尬(P < 0.001),但被认为更不便于操作(P = 0.01)和更不方便(P = 0.004)。大多数参与者由于对医疗专业知识的信任以及对程序准确性、安全性和清洁度的信心,更倾向于医生进行的筛查(84.8%)。
解释与创新
自我采集阴道样本具有高度可接受性,但医生采集的筛查仍然是首选方法。通过改善自我采集的教育、演示和支持,可能提高HPV DNA检测的参与率,并扩大菲律宾宫颈癌筛查的可及性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可能通过强调自我采样方法的教育和支持需求,为提高菲律宾HPV DNA检测的接受度并扩大筛查可及性提供策略依据。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于菲律宾的单一人群,并未考虑长期行为变化。证据是温和的,因为它是基于自我报告的看法和经验。

Acceptability and preference of self-collected vaginal samples for human papillomavirus DNA testing compared with physician-collected Pap smear.

Authors: Ourlad Alzeus G Tantengco, Mikayla Francesca N de Guzman, Amina C Kunting, Sheriah Laine M de Paz-Silava, Michele H Diwa, Ian Kim B Tabios
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Self-collected vaginal sampling for human papillomavirus (HPV) DNA testing provides a promising alternative to physician-collected Pap smears, especially in low-resource settings. However, data on its acceptability and preferences among Filipino women remain limited. This study aimed to assess women's perceptions, experiences, willingness and preferences regarding self-collected vaginal sampling for HPV DNA testing compared with Pap smears. METHODS: A cross-sectional study was conducted among 592 women. Participants underwent both physician-collected Pap smears and self-collected vaginal sampling for HPV DNA testing. Perceptions were measured using five-point Likert scale questionnaires before and after self-collection. Descriptive statistics were used, and paired and independent Wilcoxon tests were used to compare Likert responses. Willingness to undergo screening was analyzed with Cochran's Q and McNemar tests. Participants' reasons for their preferred screening method were categorized thematically and displayed in stacked bar charts. RESULTS: Self-collection was generally viewed as painless, not uncomfortable, non-embarrassing, and easy and convenient. Acceptability significantly increased after participants performed self-collection (P < 0.001 for pain and discomfort). Compared with Pap smears, self-collection was seen as less embarrassing (P < 0.001), but rated as less easy (P = 0.01) and less convenient (P = 0.004). Most participants preferred physician-administered screening (84.8%) due to trust in medical expertise, and confidence in the procedure's accuracy, safety and cleanliness. CONCLUSIONS: Self-collected vaginal sampling was highly acceptable, yet physician-collected screening remained the preferred method. Improving education, demonstrations, and support for self-collection may enhance the uptake of HPV DNA testing and broaden access to cervical cancer screening in the Philippines.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Self-collected vaginal sampling for human papillomavirus (HPV) DNA testing provides a promising alternative to physician-collected Pap smears, especially in low-resource settings. However, data on its acceptability and preferences among Filipino women remain limited.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 592 women. Participants underwent both physician-collected Pap smears and self-collected vaginal sampling for HPV DNA testing.
Core methods
Perceptions were measured using five-point Likert scale questionnaires before and after self-collection. Descriptive statistics were used, and paired and independent Wilcoxon tests were used to compare Likert responses. Willingness to undergo screening was analyzed with Cochran's Q and McNemar tests.
Main results
Self-collection was generally viewed as painless, not uncomfortable, non-embarrassing, and easy and convenient. Acceptability significantly increased after participants performed self-collection (P < 0.001 for pain and discomfort). Compared with Pap smears, self-collection was seen as less embarrassing (P < 0.001), but rated as less easy (P = 0.01) and less convenient (P = 0.004). Most participants preferred physician-administered screening (84.8%) due to trust in medical expertise, and confidence in the procedure's accuracy, safety and cleanliness.
Interpretation and novelty
Self-collected vaginal sampling was highly acceptable, yet physician-collected screening remained the preferred method. Improving education, demonstrations, and support for self-collection may enhance the uptake of HPV DNA testing and broaden access to cervical cancer screening in the Philippines.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform strategies to improve the uptake of HPV DNA testing and broaden access to cervical cancer screening in the Philippines by highlighting the need for education and support for self-collection methods.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single population in the Philippines and does not account for long-term behavioral changes. The evidence is moderate as it is based on self-reported perceptions and experiences.

治疗 后 人乳头瘤病毒 相关口咽鳞状细胞癌 Lhermitte 征的非典型表现。

Atypical presentations of Lhermitte's sign after treatment for human papillomavirus-associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma.
作者： Alifya Altaf Lokhandwala, Jonathan Paly, Thomas Roberts, Jong Chul Park, Manisha Patel, Lori Wirth, Gilbert Youssef, Jennifer L Powers, Ross D Merkin
摘要中文翻译Lhermitte 征 (LS) 的特征是颈部屈曲引起的短暂电击样感觉,沿着脊柱向下辐射,并可能延伸到腿部的后部。它是颈椎或上胸背柱功能障碍的临床指标,最常见的是由于脱髓鞘或内在脊髓病理所致。它可能发生在头颈(化疗)放射治疗后。我们描述了两名 人乳头瘤病毒 相关口咽鳞状细胞癌患者,他们在放化疗后出现 LS。一名患者在接受容量调节弧放疗和每周顺铂的确定同步放化疗完成后,出现持续 20 个月的长期 LS,另一名患者在 治疗 完成后,在辅助降级容量调节弧放疗后,出现持续 12 个月的长期 LS。这些病例显示了现代适形时代 LS 的不同病程,并强调其良性预后,但可能出现影响患者生活质量的持续症状。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关口咽鳞状细胞癌 治疗 后 Lhermitte 征 (LS) 的非典型表现,强调 LS 在这种情况下的临床相关性。
设计与对象
该研究是一项 病例报告 研究,描述了两名 HPV 相关口咽鳞状细胞癌患者在放化疗后出现 LS,包括 治疗 模式和持续时间的详细信息。
核心方法
作者记录了接受放化疗治疗 HPV 相关口咽鳞状细胞癌的患者出现 LS 的临床病例,并指出了具体的 治疗 方案和 LS 症状的持续时间。
主要结果
一名患者在完成 治疗 容量调节弧放疗和每周顺铂治疗后,出现了持续 20 个月的持久 LS,而另一名患者在辅助降级容量调节弧放疗后,LS 持续了 12 个月。
解释与创新
这些病例展示了现代适形时代 LS 的可变病程,并强调其良性预后,但可能存在影响生活质量的持续症状。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现有助于了解 HPV 相关口咽癌患者的 LS 临床表现,有助于在肿瘤学环境中识别和管理这种情况。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于 病例报告,可能无法推广到更广泛的人群,并且证据基于临床观察而不是对照试验,因此可信度中等。

Atypical presentations of Lhermitte's sign after treatment for human papillomavirus-associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma.

Authors: Alifya Altaf Lokhandwala, Jonathan Paly, Thomas Roberts, Jong Chul Park, Manisha Patel, Lori Wirth, Gilbert Youssef, Jennifer L Powers, Ross D Merkin
Original abstract (English)
Lhermitte's sign (LS) is characterised by a brief electric shock-like sensation elicited by neck flexion that radiates down the spine and may extend into the posterior aspects of the legs. It is a clinical indicator of cervical or upper thoracic dorsal column dysfunction, most commonly due to demyelination or intrinsic spinal cord pathology. It may occur after head and neck (chemo)radiotherapy. We describe two patients with human papillomavirus-associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma who developed LS following chemoradiotherapy. One patient developed protracted LS lasting 20 months beyond completion of definitive concurrent chemoradiotherapy with volumetric modulated arc radiotherapy and weekly cisplatin and another patient developed protracted LS after adjuvant de-escalated volumetric modulated arc radiotherapy lasting 12 months beyond treatment completion. These cases show the variable course of LS in the modern conformal era and highlight its benign prognosis but potential for persistent symptoms that affect patient quality of life.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to describe atypical presentations of Lhermitte's sign (LS) following treatment for human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma, highlighting the clinical relevance of LS in this context.
Design and population
The study is a case report describing two patients with HPV-associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma who developed LS after chemoradiotherapy, including details on the treatment modalities and duration.
Core methods
The authors documented clinical cases of LS in patients treated with chemoradiotherapy for HPV-associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma, noting the specific treatment protocols and the duration of LS symptoms.
Main results
One patient experienced protracted LS lasting 20 months beyond treatment completion with volumetric modulated arc radiotherapy and weekly cisplatin, while another had LS persisting for 12 months after adjuvant de-escalated volumetric modulated arc radiotherapy.
Interpretation and novelty
The cases demonstrate the variable course of LS in the modern conformal era and emphasize its benign prognosis but potential for persistent symptoms affecting quality of life.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings contribute to understanding the clinical manifestations of LS in patients with HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer, aiding in the recognition and management of this condition in oncology settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to case reports, which may not generalize to broader populations, and the evidence is based on clinical observations rather than controlled trials, resulting in moderate confidence.

基于健康信念模型的伊朗西南部女性人乳头瘤病毒预防行为的结构方程模型分析

A structural equation modeling analysis of human papillomavirus preventive behaviors among women in Southwest Iran based on the health belief model
作者： Leila Ansarifard, Sarayel Behzadinezhad, Andishe Hamedi, Abodolrahim Asadollahi, Farzaneh Ghaleh Golab, Marguerite Claire Sendall, Ali Khani Jeihooni
摘要中文翻译HPV 对 宫颈癌 的全球负担做出了重大贡献,并在伊朗西南部表现出相当大的基因型多样性。因此,了解预防行为的决定因素至关重要。鉴于预防措施的认识和采用方面持续存在差距,本研究采用健康信念模型和结构方程模型来确定影响女性参与 HPV 预防行为的心理和人口因素。对伊朗西南部 Behbahan 四个综合保健中心的妇女进行了一项横断面研究(2024-2025 年)。使用经过验证的调查问卷评估人口统计数据和 HBM 相关结构。采用验证性因素分析和结构方程模型来评估测量有效性并检查 HPV 预防行为的预测因子。在 820 名女性(平均年龄 = 36.78 ± 8.95 岁)中,感知的易感性、自我效能和感知的障碍与 HPV 预防行为显着相关。SEM 显示出出色的模型拟合度(CFI = 0.96,RMSEA = 0.04),并确定了感知易感性(β = 0.34,p < 0.001)、自我效能(β = 0.31,p < 0.001)和感知障碍(β = − 0.28,p < 0.001)对预防行为的显着影响。研究结果强调,心理因素,特别是感知的易感性、自我效能和感知的障碍,与女性参与 HPV 预防行为显着相关。针对这些因素的干预措施可能与伊朗西南部预防措施的改进有关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解伊朗西南部妇女 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 预防行为的决定因素,解决预防措施意识和采取方面的差距。
设计与对象
一项横断面研究对 2024 年至 2025 年间在伊朗西南部 Behbahan 四个综合保健中心就诊的 820 名女性进行了研究。
核心方法
使用经过验证的问卷评估人口统计数据和健康信念模型 (HBM) 相关结构,并采用结构方程模型 (SEM) 来评估测量有效性并检查 HPV 预防行为的预测因素。
主要结果
SEM 显示出出色的模型拟合度(CFI = 0.96,RMSEA = 0.04),并确定了感知易感性(β = 0.34,p < 0.001)、自我效能(β = 0.31,p < 0.001)和感知障碍(β = − 0.28,p < 0.001)对预防行为的显着影响。
解释与创新
研究结果强调,心理因素,特别是感知的易感性、自我效能和感知的障碍,与女性参与 HPV 预防行为显着相关。
科研与公卫意义
针对这些因素的干预措施可能与伊朗西南部预防措施的改善有关,从而有助于减少 HPV 相关疾病。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横截面设计限制了建立因果关系的能力,并且样本是从特定区域抽取的,这可能会影响普遍性。

A structural equation modeling analysis of human papillomavirus preventive behaviors among women in Southwest Iran based on the health belief model

Authors: Leila Ansarifard, Sarayel Behzadinezhad, Andishe Hamedi, Abodolrahim Asadollahi, Farzaneh Ghaleh Golab, Marguerite Claire Sendall, Ali Khani Jeihooni
Original abstract (English)
HPV contributes substantially to the global burden of cervical cancer and exhibits considerable genotypic diversity in southwestern Iran. Therefore, understanding the determinants of preventive behaviors is essential. Given the persistent gaps in awareness and uptake of preventive measures, this study employs the Health Belief Model and structural equation modeling to identify the psychological and demographic factors shaping women’s engagement in HPV preventive behaviors. A cross-sectional study was carried out among women attending four comprehensive health centers in Behbahan, southwest Iran (2024–2025). Demographic data and HBM-related constructs were assessed using a validated questionnaire. Confirmatory factor analysis and structural equation modeling were employed to evaluate measurement validity and examine predictors of HPV preventive behaviors. Among 820 women (mean age = 36.78 ± 8.95 years), perceived susceptibility, self-efficacy, and perceived barriers were significantly associated with HPV preventive behaviors. SEM showed excellent model fit (CFI = 0.96, RMSEA = 0.04) and identified significant effects of perceived susceptibility (β = 0.34, p < 0.001), self-efficacy (β = 0.31, p < 0.001), and perceived barriers (β = − 0.28, p < 0.001) on preventive behaviors. The findings highlight that psychological factors, particularly perceived susceptibility, self-efficacy, and perceived barriers, are significantly associated with women’s engagement in HPV preventive behaviors. Interventions addressing these factors may be associated with improved preventive practices in southwestern Iran.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the determinants of preventive behaviors against human papillomavirus (HPV) among women in southwest Iran, addressing gaps in awareness and uptake of preventive measures.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 820 women attending four comprehensive health centers in Behbahan, southwest Iran, from 2024 to 2025.
Core methods
Demographic data and Health Belief Model (HBM)-related constructs were assessed using a validated questionnaire, and structural equation modeling (SEM) was employed to evaluate measurement validity and examine predictors of HPV preventive behaviors.
Main results
SEM showed excellent model fit (CFI = 0.96, RMSEA = 0.04) and identified significant effects of perceived susceptibility (β = 0.34, p < 0.001), self-efficacy (β = 0.31, p < 0.001), and perceived barriers (β = − 0.28, p < 0.001) on preventive behaviors.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight that psychological factors, particularly perceived susceptibility, self-efficacy, and perceived barriers, are significantly associated with women’s engagement in HPV preventive behaviors.
Scientific and public-health significance
Interventions addressing these factors may be associated with improved preventive practices in southwestern Iran, contributing to the reduction of HPV-related diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s cross-sectional design limits the ability to establish causality, and the sample was drawn from a specific region, which may affect generalizability.

西孟加拉邦杜尔加布尔选定学校青少年女孩对人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 的知识和态度评估研究

A Study to Assess the Knowledge and Attitude on Human Papilloma Virus (HPV) Vaccination among Adolescents Girls in Selected School of Durgapur, West Bengal
作者： Oinam Mandakini Devi, Saharsh Walter
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是全球最常见的性传播感染之一,也是女性 宫颈癌 的主要原因。根据世界卫生组织 (WHO) 的数据,几乎所有 宫颈癌 病例均可归因于 HPV 感染,尤其是 16 型和 18 型。目的:评估青春期女孩对 HPV 疫苗接种 的了解和态度水平。确定有关 HPV 疫苗接种 的知识水平和态度之间的相关性。材料和方法:采用横断面描述性研究设计。采用有目的抽样技术招募了 100 名青春期女孩。使用自我管理的结构化问卷来收集数据。使用描述性和推论性统计数据根据研究目标对获得的数据进行制表和分析。结论:研究结果显示,大多数青春期女孩(47%)对HPV 疫苗接种的了解水平一般,44%的青春期女孩知识水平不足,9%的青春期女孩对HPV 疫苗接种的了解足够。54%的青春期女孩对HPV 疫苗接种持中立态度,43%的青春期女孩持负面态度,3%的青春期女孩对HPV 疫苗接种持赞成态度。知识和态度得分的平均值分别为25.37和24.94。得到的r值为0.701。因此,对HPV 疫苗接种的了解和态度之间存在很强的相关性。

使用了横断面描述性研究设计。采用有目的抽样技术招募了 100 名青春期女孩。

A Study to Assess the Knowledge and Attitude on Human Papilloma Virus (HPV) Vaccination among Adolescents Girls in Selected School of Durgapur, West Bengal

Authors: Oinam Mandakini Devi, Saharsh Walter
Original abstract (English)
Background: Human Papilloma virus (HPV) is one of the most common sexually transmitted infections globally and is a leading cause of cervical cancer among women. According to the World Health Organization (WHO), nearly all cases of cervical cancer can be attributed to HPV infection, particularly types 16 and 18. Objectives: To assess the level of knowledge and attitude regarding HPV vaccination among adolescent girls. To determine the co-relation between the level of knowledge and attitude regarding HPV vaccination. Material and Method: A Cross-sectional descriptive research design was used. A purposive sampling technique was used to enroll 100 adolescent girls. A Self- administered structured questionnaire was used to collect the data. Data obtained were tabulated and analyzed in terms of objectives of the study using descriptive and inferential statistics. Conclusion: The findings of the study showed that majority of adolescent girls (47%) have average level of knowledge, 44% of adolescent girls have inadequate level of knowledge and 9% of adolescent girls have adequate knowledge on HPV Vaccination. 54% of adolescent girls have neutral level of attitude, 43% adolescent girls have non-favorable level of attitude and 3% adolescent girls have favorable attitude on HPV Vaccination. The mean value of knowledge and attitude score was 25.37 and 24.94 respectively. Obtained r value is 0.701. Therefore, there was a strong correlation present between knowledge and attitude on HPV Vaccination.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human Papilloma virus (HPV) is one of the most common sexually transmitted infections globally and is a leading cause of cervical cancer among women. According to the World Health Organization (WHO), nearly all cases of cervical cancer can be attributed to HPV infection, particularly types 16 and 18.
Design and population
A Cross-sectional descriptive research design was used. A purposive sampling technique was used to enroll 100 adolescent girls.
Core methods
A Self- administered structured questionnaire was used to collect the data. Data obtained were tabulated and analyzed in terms of objectives of the study using descriptive and inferential statistics.
Main results
The findings of the study showed that majority of adolescent girls (47%) have average level of knowledge, 44% of adolescent girls have inadequate level of knowledge and 9% of adolescent girls have adequate knowledge on HPV Vaccination. 54% of adolescent girls have neutral level of attitude, 43% adolescent girls have non-favorable level of attitude and 3% adolescent girls have favorable attitude on HPV Vaccination. The mean value of knowledge and attitude score was 25.37 and 24.94 respectively. Obtained r value is 0.701.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found a strong correlation present between knowledge and attitude on HPV Vaccination.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted educational interventions to improve knowledge and attitude towards HPV vaccination among adolescent girls.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on self-reported data and may be subject to bias. The evidence is moderate as it is derived from an abstract-only source.

温州市男性门诊HPV感染基因型分布特征及短期随访

Genotype Distribution Characteristics and Short-Term Follow-Up of HPV Infection Among Male Outpatients in Wenzhou, China
作者： Xianning Dai, Chenhui Zhang, Xi Xu, Kai Xu, Zitong Qian, Xi Chen
摘要中文翻译目的:本研究旨在调查中国温州市男性门诊患者人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染的基因型分布、合并感染模式和疾病特异性特征。方法: 纳入2023年4月至2026年3月期间在温州市人民医院和温州市妇幼保健院门诊接受HPV基因分型的1934例男性门诊患者。分析了基线感染状况、临床表现、基因型共感染模式和六个月的随访结果。结果:HPV总体阳性率为39.14%。HPV52 是最常见的高危基因型(13.61%,95% CI:11.12–16.43%),最常与 HPV51 和 HPV58 共同检测,而 HPV6 是低危基因型(40.16%,95% CI:36.69–43.66%)。不同疾病组的 HPV 阳性率存在显着差异 (p < 0.001),其中在皮肤粘膜病变中观察到的阳性率最高 (58.07%,95% CI:53.89–62.18%)。单基因型感染占主导地位(73.71%,95% CI:70.58–76.85%)。757 例 HPV 阳性患者中,403 例(53.24%)完成了 6 个月的随访,其完全清除率、孤立持续率、仅事件基因型和重复感染率分别为 52.61%、28.04%、13.40% 和 5.96%。结论:这些发现强调需要针对男性的 HPV 筛查策略、针对高危人群的针对性健康教育以及按年龄分层的个体化随访方案。此类区域流行病学证据可能有助于优化男性 HPV 相关疾病的局部预防、干预和临床管理。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查中国温州男性门诊患者 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染的基因型分布、合并感染模式和疾病特异性特征。
设计与对象
纳入2023年4月至2026年3月期间在温州市人民医院和温州市妇幼保健院接受HPV基因分型的男性门诊患者1934例。
核心方法
分析了基线感染状况、临床表现、基因型共感染模式和六个月的随访结果。
主要结果
HPV总体阳性率为39.14%。HPV52 是最常见的高危基因型(13.61%,95% CI:11.12–16.43%),最常与 HPV51 和 HPV58 共同检测,而 HPV6 是低危基因型(40.16%,95% CI:36.69–43.66%)。不同疾病组的 HPV 阳性率存在显着差异 (p < 0.001),其中在皮肤粘膜病变中观察到的阳性率最高 (58.07%,95% CI:53.89–62.18%)。单基因型感染占主导地位(73.71%,95% CI:70.58–76.85%)。757 例 HPV 阳性患者中,403 例(53.24%)完成了 6 个月的随访,其完全清除率、孤立持续率、仅事件基因型和重复感染率分别为 52.61%、28.04%、13.40% 和 5.96%。
解释与创新
这些发现强调需要针对男性的 HPV 筛查策略、针对高危人群的有针对性的健康教育以及按年龄分层的个体化随访方案。
科研与公卫意义
此类区域流行病学证据可能有助于优化男性 HPV 相关疾病的局部预防、干预和临床管理。
局限与证据强度
研究设计的局限性包括潜在的选择偏倚和有限的随访期,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。证据强度中等。

Genotype Distribution Characteristics and Short-Term Follow-Up of HPV Infection Among Male Outpatients in Wenzhou, China

Authors: Xianning Dai, Chenhui Zhang, Xi Xu, Kai Xu, Zitong Qian, Xi Chen
Original abstract (English)
Objective: The aim of this study was to investigate the genotype distribution, co-infection patterns, and disease-specific characteristics of human papillomavirus (HPV) infection among male outpatients in Wenzhou, China. Methods: A total of 1934 male outpatients who underwent HPV genotyping at Wenzhou People’s Hospital and Wenzhou Maternal and Child Health Hospital between April 2023 and March 2026 were enrolled. Baseline infection status, clinical manifestations, genotype co-infection patterns, and six-month follow-up outcomes were analyzed. Results: The overall HPV positivity rate was 39.14%. HPV52 was the most prevalent high-risk genotype (13.61%, 95% CI: 11.12–16.43%), most frequently co-detected with HPV51 and HPV58, whereas HPV6 dominated low-risk genotypes (40.16%, 95% CI: 36.69–43.66%). HPV positivity differed significantly across disease groups (p < 0.001), with the highest rate observed in mucocutaneous lesions (58.07%, 95% CI: 53.89–62.18%). Single-genotype infection predominated (73.71%, 95% CI: 70.58–76.85%). Of 757 HPV-positive patients, 403 (53.24%) completed six-month follow-up, and their rates of complete clearance, isolated persistence, incident-only genotype, and superinfection were 52.61%, 28.04%, 13.40%, and 5.96%, respectively. Conclusions: These findings underscore the need for male-specific HPV screening strategies, targeted health education for high-risk populations, and age-stratified individualized follow-up protocols. Such regional epidemiological evidence may help optimize local prevention, intervention, and clinical management of HPV-associated disorders in men.

肛门生殖器疣患者特定性传播感染(HBV、HCV、HIV 和梅毒)的血清学标志物:一项两中心、为期五年的研究

Serological Markers of Selected Sexually Transmitted Infections (HBV, HCV, HIV, and Syphilis) in Patients with Anogenital Warts: A Two-Centre, Five-Year Study
作者： Aziz Alper Biten, Meryem Çetin, S. Süel, Ömer Kutlu, Hakan Şıvgın, Şirin Çetin
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 引起的肛门生殖器粘膜屏障破坏可能会增加对其他性传播感染 (STI) 的易感性,指南建议对肛门生殖器疣 (AGW) 患者进行 STI 筛查。
设计与对象
在这项回顾性横断面研究中,对 2020 年 12 月至 2025 年 10 月期间临床诊断为 AGW 的所有连续患者 (n = 779) 进行了评估。
核心方法
血清流行率 使用 Wilson 95% 置信区间 (CI) 进行估计,使用实际测试的患者数量作为分母。描述了多种反应性血清学标记的模式,并使用 Cochran-Armitage 趋势检验检查了年龄和血清阳性之间的关系。使用带有 Woolf logit CI 的 Fisher 精确检验和补充的 Firth 惩罚逻辑回归探讨了人口统计学因素与伴随的 STI 血清阳性的关联;鉴于事件数量较少,这些分析被认为是探索性的和假设生成的。
主要结果
Among 779 patients (585 [75%] male; mean age, 35.80 ± 12.32 years), the overall seroprevalence of concomitant STIs was 2.4% (19/779; 95% CI, 1.6–3.8). Seroprevalence was 1.4% for HBsAg, 0.9% for anti-HCV, 0.2% for anti-HIV, and 1.6% for treponemal antibodies, while anti-HBs immunity was present in 53.5% of patients. Two patients (10.5% of seropositive cases) had more than one concomitant STI. Seropositivity increased from 0.7% in patients aged 17–24 years to 3.5% in those aged ≥45 years, although the trend was not statistically significant (p = 0.13).
解释与创新
AGW患者中测试标志物的血清流行率较低但不可忽略,并且随着年龄的增长而增加,而与当前感染更一致的标志物亚组不太常见。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持指南建议的所有 AGW 患者的血清学筛查(包括乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 评估),并且鉴于血清阳性病例数量有限,应被视为假设生成。
局限与证据强度
鉴于血清阳性病例数量较少,所探讨的关联被认为是探索性和假设生成性的,并且研​​究设计是回顾性的,这可能会引入偏差并限制普遍性。

Serological Markers of Selected Sexually Transmitted Infections (HBV, HCV, HIV, and Syphilis) in Patients with Anogenital Warts: A Two-Centre, Five-Year Study

Authors: Aziz Alper Biten, Meryem Çetin, S. Süel, Ömer Kutlu, Hakan Şıvgın, Şirin Çetin
Original abstract (English)
Background: Human papillomavirus-induced disruption of the anogenital mucosal barrier may increase susceptibility to other sexually transmitted infections (STIs), and guidelines recommend STI screening in patients with anogenital warts (AGW). We aimed to determine the seroprevalence of concomitant STIs in patients with AGW over a five-year period and to perform an exploratory assessment of associated demographic factors. Methods: In this retrospective, cross-sectional study, all consecutive patients clinically diagnosed with AGW between December 2020 and October 2025 (n = 779) were evaluated. Serological results for HBsAg, anti-HBs, anti-HCV, anti-HIV, and syphilis (treponemal and non-treponemal) were obtained. Seroprevalence was estimated with Wilson 95% confidence intervals (CIs) using the number of patients actually tested as the denominator. Patterns of multiple reactive serological markers were described, and the relationship between age and seropositivity was examined using a Cochran–Armitage trend test. Associations of demographic factors with concomitant STI seropositivity were explored using Fisher’s exact test with Woolf logit CIs and a complementary Firth penalized logistic regression; given the small number of events, these analyses were considered exploratory and hypothesis-generating. Results: Among 779 patients (585 [75%] male; mean age, 35.80 ± 12.32 years), the overall seroprevalence of concomitant STIs was 2.4% (19/779; 95% CI, 1.6–3.8). Seroprevalence was 1.4% for HBsAg, 0.9% for anti-HCV, 0.2% for anti-HIV, and 1.6% for treponemal antibodies, while anti-HBs immunity was present in 53.5% of patients. Two patients (10.5% of seropositive cases) had more than one concomitant STI. Seropositivity increased from 0.7% in patients aged 17–24 years to 3.5% in those aged ≥45 years, although the trend was not statistically significant (p = 0.13). Anti-HBs negativity was associated with concomitant STI seropositivity (OR = 5.63; 95% CI, 1.60–19.83; p = 0.003); this association was attenuated and no longer statistically significant after excluding HBV markers (OR = 2.73; 95% CI, 0.70–10.69; p = 0.200). A complete-case analysis restricted to patients with a fully defined testing panel yielded a similar prevalence estimate (2.8%; 95% CI, 1.7–4.5). Conclusions: The seroprevalence of the tested markers in patients with AGW was low but non-negligible and increased with age, while the subgroup of markers more consistent with current infection was less common. Anti-HBs negativity was associated with the composite outcome, but a sensitivity analysis showed this was largely attributable to its expected link with HBV status. These findings support guideline-recommended serological screening—including hepatitis B vaccination assessment—in all patients with AGW, and should be regarded as hypothesis-generating given the limited number of seropositive cases.

Limitations and evidence strength
